We also identify and correct a degenerate curvature estimator, analyze the robustness of the proposed estimator, and explicitly discuss the limitations of the method, including Hessian staleness in non-convex optimization. These results establish CWGD as a principled geometry-aware measure of optimization noise and motivate future extensions to more general learning problems.

</details>


### [182] [HSAP: A Hierachical Sequence-aware Parallelism for Hybrid-Context Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30460)
*Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Zhuoyang Song,Cong lin,Junyu Lu,Jiaxing Zhang,Bingyi Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种序列感知并行算法（Sequence-Aware Parallelism），通过JIT编译优化通信策略，并构建分层序列感知并行框架，解决了混合上下文打包序列中因果注意力计算错误的问题，在多个指标上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列并行范式无法正确处理混合上下文打包序列上的因果注意力计算，导致交叉污染问题；同时现有方法要么忽略混合上下文场景，要么牺牲并行度来支持该场景。

Method: 提出序列感知并行算法，利用JIT编译优化NCCL级别的多设备组通信策略，克服密集张量传输和部分注意力计算障碍；将现有序列并行范式整合为分层序列感知并行框架，并优化内存和通信开销管理。

Result: 在多个实验指标上，所提出方法优于其他最先进的序列并行方法。

Conclusion: 提出的序列感知并行算法和分层框架有效解决了混合上下文序列的因果注意力正确计算问题，同时保持了较高的并行效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim to combine the advantages of existing sequence parallelism paradigms and overcomes their drawbacks, the most serious of which is the incapability to correctly compute causal attention on the hybrid-context packed sequences, in a stronger sequence parallelism framework. The practical technique of packing sequences for efficiently pretraining and fine-tuning large language models causes cross-contamination problem in attention computation, which can be effectively solved when no parallelism in the sequence length dimension is taken. However, in sequence parallelism, existing approaches either ignore the scenario of hybrid-context sequences or conversely sacrifice and limit parallelism degree for supporting the scenario. To this end, we innovatively propose an efficient Sequence-Aware Parallelism algorithm to conquer the obstacles of intensive tensor transmission and partial attention computation across multiple device groups. Our algorithm utilizes JIT (Just-In-Time) compilation to optimize the communication strategy of all device groups in NCCL level. Further, we integrate existing sequence parallelism paradigms into a Hierachical Sequence-Aware Parallelism framework which benefits from our sequence-aware algorithm. We additionally elaborate on the memory and communication overhead management of the hierachical framework to optimize its performance. Through multiple experiments, we demonstrate that our proposed approach outperform other state-of-the-arts sequence parallelism approches in multiple metrics.

</details>


### [183] [MuonSSM: Orthogonalizing State Space Models for Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30461)
*Thai-Khanh Nguyen,Ngoc-Bich-Uyen Vo,Thieu N. Vo,Tan M. Nguyen,Cuong Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonSSM通过动量路径和低秩牛顿-舒尔茨变换来条件化状态空间模型的更新几何，从而解决长序列建模中的不稳定性和记忆退化问题，在语言、视觉和时间序列任务上取得了稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的状态空间模型（SSM）在处理长序列时，由于一阶更新矩阵条件数差和更新几何不平衡，常常出现训练不稳定和记忆退化问题。

Method: 提出MuonSSM框架，通过显式条件化记忆更新的几何结构（而非直接约束循环转移矩阵）来稳定SSM训练。具体包含两个关键组件：动量路径（momentum-based pathway）和轻量级牛顿-舒尔茨变换（Newton-Schulz transformation），作用于低秩输入注入上，产生有界且谱条件良好的更新，同时保持并行扫描复杂度。

Result: 在语言、视觉和时间序列基准测试中，集成到多种SSM骨干网络后，在准确性、鲁棒性和长上下文性能上均取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: 更新几何的条件化（geometric conditioning of updates）是实现稳定、可扩展序列建模的原则性途径。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs) have emerged as efficient linear-time alternatives to attention for long-sequence modeling. However, existing SSMs often suffer from instability and memory degradation over extended horizons due to poorly conditioned first-order updates and unbalanced update geometry. We introduce MuonSSM, a general framework that stabilizes SSM training by explicitly conditioning the geometry of memory updates rather than the recurrent transition matrix. MuonSSM augments SSMs with a momentum-based pathway and a lightweight Newton Schulz transformation on low-rank input injections, yielding bounded and spectrally conditioned updates while preserving parallel scan complexity. Theory shows that MuonSSM improves gradient propagation, mitigates spectral amplification, and enriches memory representations over long horizons. Extensive experiments across language, vision, and time-series benchmarks show consistent gains in accuracy, robustness, and long-context performance when integrated into diverse SSM backbones. These results establish geometric conditioning of updates as a principled pathway to stable, scalable sequence modeling.

</details>


### [184] [Discovering Collaboration from Novelty: Random Network Distillation for Clustered Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30499)
*Davide Domini,Gianluca Aguzzi,Ivana Dusparic,Danilo Pianini,Mirko Viroli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于随机网络蒸馏的轻量级聚类联邦学习方法，通过客户端本地训练RND预测器生成的新颖性信号来估算客户端之间的相似度，在联邦训练前即可发现有意义的客户端分组，从而解耦聚类与学习过程，适用于非独立同分布数据下的自主大规模分布式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非独立同分布数据下性能退化，单一的全局模型难以表征客户端分布的多样性。现有聚类联邦学习方法将聚类分配与主训练循环耦合，导致计算和通信开销增加。

Method: 每个客户端在其本地数据上训练一个轻量级的随机网络蒸馏（RND）预测器，利用预测误差作为新颖性信号来估计与其他客户端的相似度，从而在联邦训练之前发现有意义的客户端分组，实现聚类与主模型学习的解耦。

Result: 该方法能够在运行时从局部新颖性估计中自发形成联邦分组，无需预先指定聚类数量或协作结构，实现了任务无关且高效的自主协作机制。

Conclusion: 通过将聚类与学习过程解耦，该方法为大规模分布式系统在非独立同分布数据下提供了一种轻量级、任务无关且高效的自主协作联邦学习方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning often suffers under non-independently and identically distributed data, where a single global model may fail to represent the diversity of client distributions. Clustered Federated Learning mitigates this issue by training specialized models for groups of similar clients, but existing approaches often couple cluster assignment with the main training loop, increasing computational and communication costs. We propose a lightweight clustering approach based on Random Network Distillation. Each client trains a compact Random Network Distillation predictor on its local data and uses its prediction error as a novelty signal to estimate similarity with other clients. This enables the discovery of meaningful client groups before federated training, without sharing raw data or repeatedly evaluating the main model. Crucially, the resulting federations emerge from local novelty estimates at runtime, making the method suitable for autonomous large-scale distributed systems where neither the number of clusters nor the collaboration structure can be specified a priori. Overall, by decoupling clustering from learning, the method provides a task-agnostic and efficient mechanism for autonomous collaboration under non-independently and identically distributed data.

</details>


### [185] [Muon learns balanced solutions in matrix factorization without slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30509)
*Mark Rhee,Jamie Simon,Dhruva Karkada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器与梯度下降在矩阵分解问题中的动力学差异，发现Muon具有三点主要区别：避免慢速鞍点间动力学、超越临界学习率稳定性、以及不同的守恒量。作者利用Muon的结构特性设计了能在两步优化中实现近完美对齐的学习率调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Muon优化器在非线性参数动力学和表示学习场景下与梯度下降的根本差异，揭示其加速收敛的内在机制。

Method: 理论分析矩阵分解问题中Muon与梯度下降的动力学轨迹；推导简单设置下的对齐速率；利用Muon结构特性构造学习率调度。

Result: Muon在小初始化下同时学习所有主模式且小模式先收敛；学习率可超过局部损失尖锐度的临界阈值而不发散；Muon流守恒\sqrt{P^TP}-\sqrt{Q^TQ}而非梯度流的P^TP-Q^TQ；设计了仅需两步优化实现近完美对齐的学习率调度。

Conclusion: Muon通过不同的动力学机制（避免鞍点、稳定大学习率、独特守恒量）实现快速收敛，其结构特性可用于设计高效的学习率调度策略。

Abstract: Matrix factorization (i.e., problems of the form $\min_{\mathbf{P},\mathbf{Q}} \|\mathbf{M}^\star - \mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{Q}\|_\mathrm{F}^2$) is a minimal learning problem that exhibits both nonlinear parameter dynamics and representation learning. In this setting, we study how parameter trajectories under the Muon optimizer differ from those of gradient descent. We identify three main dynamical differences: 1) Muon avoids the slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics from small initialization. Muon instead learns all the top modes of $\mathbf{M}^\star$ at the same rate, with the smaller modes converging first. 2) Muon remains stable even when the learning rate exceeds the critical threshold set by the local loss sharpness. This frees the learning rate from the condition number of the problem, enabling rapid convergence via exponential learning rate annealing. 3) Once the weights are aligned with each other and the target, Muon flow conserves the matrix quantity $\sqrt{\mathbf{P}^\top \mathbf{P}}-\sqrt{\mathbf{Q}^\top \mathbf{Q}}$, while gradient flow is known to conserve the matrix $\mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{P} - \mathbf{Q}^\top\mathbf{Q}$. Despite having distinct conserved quantities, both optimizers find the so-called \textit{balanced} solution from vanishing initialization. When training from small random initialization, the weights spontaneously align early in training. We derive the alignment rates in simple settings and show that they predict the empirical alignment rates in general. Finally, we exploit structural properties of Muon to construct a learning rate schedule that achieves near-perfect alignment in only two optimization steps.

</details>


### [186] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵学习地平线（ELH）统一框架，将信息论、拓扑学和统计力学联系起来，解释了过参数化深度网络的泛化能力。通过香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，揭示了当目标边界的几何复杂度超过信息地平线时，系统会经历熵相变进入信息挫折状态（玻璃态记忆阶段），导致泛化在热力学上不可能。同时将"顿悟"现象解释为熵释放，并提出了熵梯度下降（EGD）算法来动态管理权重熵以维持学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论（VC维、Rademacher复杂度）无法解释过参数化深度网络的惊人泛化能力，理论与现实之间存在巨大鸿沟。本文旨在通过统一信息论、拓扑学和统计力学来弥合这一差距，揭示深度学习的基本极限。

Method: 提出了熵学习地平线（ELH）作为基本定律：网络能真正学习目标函数的条件是数据流形的香农熵超过目标函数决策边界的拓扑熵，且与权重空间的冯·诺依曼熵相平衡。建立了香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，证明当边界几何复杂度超过信息地平线时发生熵相变，进入信息挫折（玻璃态记忆）阶段。将顿悟解释为熵释放。提出熵梯度下降（EGD）算法动态管理权重熵。

Result: 理论上证明了存在一个信息地平线，当目标函数的几何复杂度超过该界限时，泛化在热力学上不可能。将顿悟现象重新解释为权重的突然熵重组。提出了可实践的EGD优化算法。

Conclusion: 熵不仅是追踪不确定性的工具，更是决定机器能否学习的根本物理量。该工作重新定位了熵在深度学习中的核心地位。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>


### [187] [ITSPACE: Monotone Gaussian Optimal Transport Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30523)
*Woojoo Na,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ITSPACE算法，通过近端主化-最小化方法直接优化协方差矩阵间的精确Bures-Wasserstein目标，利用平方根分解实现闭式更新，在保持PSD结构的同时显著加速协方差对齐任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵作为特征分布的紧凑描述子在领域自适应和高斯嵌入中广泛使用，但现有方法（如BW梯度下降、熵正则化OT、其他协方差几何方法）在优化精确Bures-Wasserstein目标时收敛慢或不精确，亟需一种轻量级、可迭代并保持正定结构的优化方案。

Method: 提出ITSPACE算法，采用近端主化-最小化框架，在平方根因式分解上执行闭式更新，每次迭代保证BW目标充分下降；针对非精确极分解情形，给出显式的证书间隔界来量化与控制偏离程度；通过构造性方式保持PSD结构并天然支持秩限制因子。

Result: 在多个真实世界协方差对齐基准上，ITSPACE在达到低BW间隔解的速度上显著优于BW梯度下降、其他协方差几何方法以及熵正则化样本OT基线。

Conclusion: ITSPACE作为一种轻量级内循环原语，适用于在严格步数和计算预算下从无标签目标批次执行适应的场景，兼顾理论保证与实用效率。

Abstract: Covariance matrices serve as compact descriptors of feature distributions in many machine-learning pipelines, including domain adaptation and Gaussian embeddings. Under a centered Gaussian approximation, the unregularized Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport (OT) discrepancy admits a closed form on covariances given by the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) objective on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) cone. We propose ITSPACE (Iterative Transport for Stable Proximal Alignment of Covariance Embeddings), a proximal majorization-minimization method that directly optimizes this exact BW objective through closed-form updates in a square-root factorization. In exact arithmetic, each iteration satisfies a sufficient-decrease inequality for the BW objective; under inexact polar computations, we provide an explicit certificate-gap bound controlling deviations from exact descent. The resulting iterations preserve PSD structure by construction and naturally support rank-restricted factors, making ITSPACE well-suited as a lightweight inner-loop primitive in settings where adaptation must be performed from unlabeled target batches under strict step and compute budgets. Across real-world covariance-alignment benchmarks, ITSPACE reaches low-BW-gap solutions substantially faster than BW-gradient descent, methods based on other covariance geometries, and entropically regularized sample-OT baselines.

</details>


### [188] [TraceLab: Characterizing Coding Agent Workloads for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30560)
*Kan Zhu,Mathew Jacob,Chenxi Ma,Yi Pan,Stephanie Wang,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Baris Kasikci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文收集并发布了约4,300个编码智能体会话轨迹（含35万LLM步骤和43万工具调用），分析发现编码智能体工作负载具有长自主循环、长上下文短输出、多样化长尾工具调用、高但不完美的前缀缓存命中率等特征，为优化服务系统（如降低工具调用开销、感知追加长度的预填充、语义感知工具延迟预测、改进KV缓存管理）提供了具体方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正成为大语言模型智能体的重要应用场景，但其高效服务面临挑战。现有公开轨迹和基准无法捕捉真实日常编码智能体使用模式，缺乏支持服务系统分析的多智能体、多模型家族数据。

Method: 收集了来自日常使用Claude Code和Codex的约4,300个编码智能体会话轨迹（含35万LLM步骤和43万工具调用），通过分析这些轨迹来揭示实际工作负载特征。

Result: 编码智能体工作负载具有四大特征：长自主循环、长上下文短输出、多样化长尾工具调用、高但不完美的前缀缓存命中率。

Conclusion: 这些发现指出了具体的服务优化机会，包括降低工具调用开销、实现感知追加长度的预填充、语义感知工具延迟预测以及围绕人类节奏间隙改进KV缓存管理。

Abstract: Coding agents are rapidly becoming a major application of agentic LLMs, but serving them efficiently remains challenging. Progress on this challenge requires understanding real workload patterns, yet the data needed for such analysis is largely absent. Existing public traces and benchmarks do not capture real, day-to-day coding-agent usage across multiple agents and model families for serving-system analysis. To help fill this gap, we collect and release a trace of roughly 4,300 coding-agent sessions, containing about 350,000 LLM steps and 430,000 tool calls from our own day-to-day use of Claude Code and Codex. Our analysis shows that coding-agent workloads feature long autonomous loops, long contexts with short outputs, diverse and heavily-tailed tool calls, and high but imperfect prefix cache hit rates. These findings point to concrete opportunities for optimizing serving, including lower-overhead tool calling, append-length-aware prefill, semantic-aware tool-latency prediction, and improved KV-cache management around human-paced gaps. We release the dataset, trace collection pipeline, and analysis code at https://github.com/uw-syfi/TraceLab.git; the project website is https://tracelab.cs.washington.edu.

</details>


### [189] [Attractor States Emerge in Multi-Turn LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30571)
*Ting-Wen Ko,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究LLM在多智能体开放对话中是否表现出吸引子行为，发现自洽对话具有模型特定的吸引子，在混合对话中不对称地影响其他模型的风格和立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于开放式的多智能体场景，但模型间长期互动的动态机制尚不明确，特别是是否存在类似吸引子的稳定行为模式。

Method: 在7种LLM和20个争议性话题上，进行自洽和混合配对的双人辩论，追踪表征空间轨迹、话语特征和立场变化，分析吸引子效应及其不对称性。

Result: 自洽对话轨迹呈现模型特定的吸引子；混合对话中吸引子不对称地影响对方模型的风格和行为，如Claude Haiku是强吸引子，GPT-4.1 nano易受影响。

Conclusion: 开放LLM交互的部分可预测性源于模型特定吸引子，但受结构化、不对称的伙伴影响塑造，为设计、预测和监控自主智能体系统提供启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in open-ended multi-agent settings, but the long-run dynamics of model--model interaction remain poorly understood. We study whether open-ended LLM discussions exhibit attractor-like behavior, i.e. topic-independent stable sets of behaviors which conversations settle into. Across 7 LLMs and 20 controversial topics, we compare self-play and mixed-play dyadic debates, tracking trajectories in representation space, discourse traits, and stances. We find self-play trajectories to be model-specific attractors that draw their conversation partners asymmetrically in mixed-play debates, influencing the other models' stylistic choices and behavior. For example, Claude Haiku is a strong attractor of other models in latent space, corresponding to other models taking on its traits like metacommentary, and models like GPT-4.1 nano are especially malleable. Our results suggest that open-ended LLM interactions are partially predictable from model-specific attractors, but shaped by structured and asymmetric partner influence. Overall, our analysis sheds some light on the complex behavior of open-ended multi-agent interaction, which we hope is helpful in designing, predicting, and monitoring autonomous agentic systems in the real world.

</details>


### [190] [SWE-INTERACT: Reimagining SWE Benchmarks as User-Driven Long-Horizon Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30573)
*Mohit Raghavendra,Anisha Gunjal,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Interact是一个评估编码智能体在多轮、交互式、用户驱动软件工程任务中的新测试平台。与一次性提供完整需求的现有基准不同，它通过用户模拟器逐步揭示需求、提供反馈，测试智能体发现用户意图、适应变化需求的能力。实验表明，单轮任务表现好不意味着多轮交互任务表现好，最强模型在SWE-Interact上仅解决约25%的任务，揭示了当前模型在交互式目标发现与迭代精炼方面的短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE基准测试（如SWE-bench）通常一次性提供完整需求，评估智能体的自主实现能力，这无法反映真实开发场景中的交互式、渐进式需求澄清过程。研究者希望通过构建一个模拟真实开发者工作流的测试平台，评估智能体在多轮交互中理解用户意图、适应需求变化的能力。

Method: 构建SWE-Interact测试平台，包含一个精心设计的用户模拟器，该模拟器从模糊或不完整的指令开始，逐步揭示需求，检查智能体工作区，提供针对性反馈、修订和新约束，直到完整任务目标被传达。基于对真实编码智能体交互的大规模研究设计交互流程。在多个前沿和开源模型上进行评估，对比单轮基线任务与多轮SWE-Interact任务的性能。

Result: 最强模型在单轮基线任务中解决约50%，但在对应的SWE-Interact多轮任务中仅解决约25%。Opus 4.8和GPT 5.5等最强模型在面对模糊初始指令时表现良好，能坚持到所有需求被揭示，整合需求更好且代码更整洁，但仍存在过度主动编码、遗忘需求和技术错误等问题。较弱模型在模糊情况下起步差、过早放弃、遗忘或忽略指令、更频繁重写代码。

Conclusion: SWE-Interact衡量了现有基准未覆盖的正交能力维度——交互式目标发现与用户参与下的迭代精炼。强性能在单轮任务上不能可靠迁移到多轮用户驱动工作流，表明当前模型在真实交互式软件工程场景中存在显著不足，需要进一步开发。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Interact, a new testbed for evaluating coding agents on multi-turn, interactive, user-driven software engineering tasks. Existing frontier SWE benchmarks typically provide complete requirements upfront and evaluate agents on autonomous implementation. In contrast, SWE-Interact places agents in a realistic developer workflow: a carefully designed user simulator starts with vague or incomplete instructions, progressively reveals requirements, inspects the agent's workspace, and provides targeted feedback, revisions, and new constraints until the full task goal has been handed off. Grounded in large-scale studies of real coding-agent interactions, this setup tests whether agents can discover user intent, adapt to evolving requirements, and build on their own prior work. Across a suite of frontier and open-weight models, we find that strong performance on single-turn SWE tasks does not reliably transfer to multi-turn, user-driven workflows: the best-performing models solve roughly 50% of single-turn baseline tasks but only 25% of the corresponding SWE-Interact tasks. The strongest models in our evaluation, including Opus 4.8 and GPT 5.5, start strong even in the face of vague initial instructions, persevere until all the requirements are surfaced by the user, integrate them better and write clean code. However, they still suffer from over-agentic coding, forgetting requirements and technical mistakes. Weaker models start poorly under ambiguity, give up early, forget or ignore instructions and rework their code more. Overall, SWE-Interact measures an orthogonal, real-world capability axis for frontier model development: interactive goal discovery and iterative refinement with a user in the loop.

</details>


### [191] [The Fundamental Limits of Valid Transport Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30574)
*Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在极小极大框架下正式定义了任意有效传输映射的估计问题，指出在标准稳定性假设下，估计任意传输映射与估计最优传输映射在统计上难度相同；但当稳定性假设不成立时，替代传输映射可以比最优传输映射学习得更准确。这为理解基于传输的生成方法的统计极限提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现代生成模型（如扩散模型、归一化流、流匹配）估计的是分布之间的传输映射或计划，但并未明确针对最优传输（OT）映射。在实际应用中传输成本本身并不重要，因此自然希望估计从统计或计算角度更易处理的映射。本文旨在严格形式化这一任务，建立其统计极限。

Method: 采用极小极大（minimax）框架，将估计任意有效传输映射的问题形式化；结合最优传输理论中的稳定性假设（如强凸性、光滑性等标准假设），推导样本复杂度下界；并通过反例展示当稳定性假设失效时的情形。

Result: 在标准的OT稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射与估计OT映射具有相同的样本复杂度下界，即两者统计难度相当。当稳定性假设不成立时，存在替代传输映射可以显著比OT映射学习得更准确。

Conclusion: 极小极大框架为理解基于传输的现代生成方法的统计极限提供了严格基础，阐明了在何种情况下追求次优映射能够带来真正的统计优势。

Abstract: Many modern generative modeling methods, including diffusion models, normalizing flows, and flow matching, estimate transport maps or plans between distributions without explicitly targeting an optimal transport (OT) map. In applications like generative modeling, the transport cost itself is irrelevant, and this makes it natural to target maps which are more tractable from either a statistical or computational standpoint. In this short note, we formalize the task of estimating any valid transport map in a rigorous minimax framework. One consequence of this framing is that it yields sample complexity lower bounds for any method whose learned object is evaluated as a transport map or plan, including flow matching and diffusion-based generative models, in settings where direct analysis would be challenging due to the analytic complexity of the methods and their target maps. We observe that, under standard, though strong, stability assumptions from the OT literature, estimating any valid transport map is statistically as hard as estimating the OT map. We complement these results with some examples showing that when these stability assumptions fail, alternative transport maps can be learned substantially more accurately than the OT map. Our minimax framing provides a rigorous foundation for understanding the statistical limits of modern transport-based generative methods and clarifies when targeting sub-optimal maps can provide real statistical advantages.

</details>


### [192] [C$^{2}$R: Cross-sample Consistency Regularization Mitigates Feature Splitting and Absorption in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30609)
*Haoran Jin,Xiting Wang,Shijie Ren,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏自编码器（SAE）在扩展字典时出现的特征分裂和特征吸收问题，提出跨样本一致性正则化（C²R）方法，通过惩罚批次中方向相似潜变量的共激活，确保语义特征被统一潜变量一致表示，从而提升潜变量可解释性且不损害重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE在扩展到大字典时存在特征分裂（将连贯概念碎片化为非原子潜变量）和特征吸收（在通用特征中产生任意例外）问题，严重损害潜变量可靠性。这些问题的根源在于缺乏跨样本约束，导致同一语义概念在不同样本中被不一致地分配到多个冗余或干扰潜变量上。

Method: 提出跨样本一致性正则化（C²R），通过惩罚批次中方向相似潜变量的共激活，显式鼓励每个语义特征被统一的潜变量一致地表示，从而引入跨样本约束来稳定潜变量分配。

Result: C²R有效缓解了特征分裂和特征吸收问题，同时保持了重建保真度，提升了潜变量可解释性且不降低模型性能。

Conclusion: C²R为SAE的潜变量不一致分配问题提供了原则性解决方案，在不牺牲重建质量的前提下增强了可解释性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret large language models by decomposing activations into sparse, human-understandable features, but scaling to large dictionaries exposes fundamental challenges. Systematic studies reveal pervasive feature splitting that fragments coherent concepts into non-atomic latents and widespread feature absorption that creates arbitrary exceptions in general features, severely compromising latent reliability. These issues stem from inconsistent latent assignment across samples: without cross-sample constraints, per-sample optimization often allows a single underlying concept to be inconsistently distributed across multiple redundant or interfering latents. To address this, we introduce C$^2$R (\underline{\textbf{C}}ross-sample \underline{\textbf{C}}onsistency \underline{\textbf{R}}egularization). C$^2$R explicitly encourages that each semantic feature is consistently represented by a unified latent across the batch by penalizing the co-activation of directionally similar latents. Comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that C$^2$R effectively mitigates both splitting and absorption while, crucially, preserving reconstruction fidelity, providing a principled solution that enhances latent interpretability without degrading model performance. Source code is available at https://github.com/hr-jin/Cross-sample-Consistency-Regularization.

</details>


### [193] [Pessimism's Paradox: Conservative Offline Training Amplifies Reward Hacking During Online Adaptation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30627)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，更高的离线保守性（高β DPO）会单调增加在线奖励破解（reward hacking）的损害，原因是高β压缩了策略熵，降低了响应多样性，使得集成不确定性被更快利用。研究拟合了功率律曲线并提出了一个平衡对齐保真度与破解脆弱性的最优保守水平β*，主张需要"校准的"而非"最大化的"保守主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"离线训练越保守，在线适应越安全"这一广泛直觉，揭示保守性反而可能加剧奖励破解问题的机制。

Method: 在Qwen3-14B上使用DPO训练三个保守性水平的策略（β_lo, β_mid, β_hi），然后对各检查点在线适配一个奖励集成（3×Qwen3-1.7B），在GSM8K上测量真实准确率的Goodhart差距及曲线下面积（AUGC），并进行机制分析。

Result: 更高离线保守性单调增加奖励破解损伤（Spearman ρ=1.0）；高β压缩策略熵→低多样性响应（低成对余弦距离）→集成不确定性增加且被更快利用。

Conclusion: 需要校准的保守性而非最大化的保守性，存在一个最优β*平衡对齐保真度与破解脆弱性。

Abstract: Conservative offline training is widely advocated as a safe foundation for subsequent online adaptation: if a policy stays close to well-supported behaviour, the argument goes, it is less likely to exploit imperfections in a learned reward model. We challenge this intuition empirically and mechanistically. We train a Qwen3-14B policy under Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) with three levels of conservatism ($β\in \{β_{\mathrm{lo}}, β_{\mathrm{mid}}, β_{\mathrm{hi}}\}$ derived from empirical log-ratio percentiles), then adapt each checkpoint online against a learned reward ensemble (3\,$\times$\,Qwen3-1.7B) while measuring true performance on GSM8K exact-answer accuracy. We find that \emph{higher offline conservatism monotonically increases reward-hacking damage}, measured by the Goodhart gap and its area under the curve (AUGC), with Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ across all three conditions. Mechanistic analysis reveals a three-link causal chain: (i) high-$β$ DPO compresses policy entropy, (ii) Low-entropy policies generate responses with reduced diversity, concentrating in a narrow region of the reward model's training distribution (lower pairwise cosine distance), and (iii) despite this proximity, ensemble disagreement (epistemic uncertainty) increases with $β$ and is exploited faster during online optimisation. We further fit a power-law curve to the $(β, \augc)$ data and identify a practical optimal conservatism level $β^{\star}$ that balances alignment fidelity against hacking vulnerability. Our results suggest that the field needs \emph{calibrated}, not \emph{maximal}, conservatism.

</details>


### [194] [One-Step Gradient Delay is Not a Barrier for Large-Scale Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30634)
*Philip Zmushko,Egor Petrov,Nursultan Abdullaev,Mikhail Khrushchev,Samuel Horváth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现异步流水线并行中的一步梯度延迟问题并非固有缺陷，而是依赖优化器选择。AdamW在此场景下表现差，但Muon等新优化器具有鲁棒性。作者提出基于错误反馈的修正方法，在10B参数模型上验证了与同步训练相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步流水线并行（如PipeDream-2BW）虽能消除流水线气泡、最大化吞吐量，但由于梯度陈旧性问题被认为训练不稳定，实际采用受限。本文旨在挑战这一假设，证明一步延迟下的性能退化并非不可克服。

Method: 首先进行全面的实证分析，比较不同优化器（AdamW vs Muon）在一步梯度延迟下的表现；其次提出一种与优化器无关的基于错误反馈（Error Feedback）的修正方法以缓解延迟影响；最后提供理论分析证明Muon在有/无该修正下的收敛性。

Result: AdamW在一阶梯延迟下性能严重退化，而Muon表现出强鲁棒性；所提出的错误反馈修正进一步缩小了与同步训练的差距；在高达10B参数的模型上验证了策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 异步流水线并行在大规模训练中具有实际应用潜力，通过正确选择优化器（如Muon）并辅以适当的修正方法，可以在不牺牲模型质量的前提下实现高吞吐量训练。

Abstract: Modern large-scale LLM pretraining benefits from utilizing Pipeline Parallelism; however, synchronous implementations leave GPUs idle during pipeline bubbles, wasting computational resources. Asynchronous Pipeline Parallelism eliminates these bubbles, maximizing throughput at the cost of gradient staleness. Among asynchronous schedules, PipeDream-2BW is particularly appealing: unlike the original PipeDream schedule, it ensures a constant one-step gradient delay regardless of pipeline depth. However, its adoption remains limited due to the common belief that optimizing under staleness is fundamentally unstable. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that degradation under one-step delay depends strongly on optimizer choice rather than being an intrinsic limitation. We provide the first comprehensive empirical analysis showing that while AdamW, the predominant optimizer at the time when PipeDream-2BW was introduced, indeed suffers from severe degradation, recent methods like Muon exhibit strong robustness under a one-step delay. We introduce an optimizer-agnostic Error Feedback-inspired correction to further mitigate delay effects. We provide supporting theoretical analysis demonstrating convergence for Muon with and without this correction. Extensive evaluation on models up to 10B parameters confirms that our strategies bridge the performance gap with synchronous training, highlighting the practical potential of asynchronous pipeline parallelism at scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSEA（递归自进化智能体），通过三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、规程）在无权重更新的条件下实现智能体自进化。在四个基准测试上的实验表明：无单一方法普遍最优；无保护的上下文进化高方差且不安全；RSEA的严格保留筛选机制确保了递归自进化的单调安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体的无权重更新改进方法（如反射、工作流、提示优化等）通常只在单个基准上报告正向结果，缺乏跨方法的公平、系统对比，且未充分研究无保护上下文进化带来的风险。

Method: 提出RSEA框架，维护三层自然语言状态：命令性策略（imperative strategy）、可复用技能（reusable skills）和过程性规程（procedural playbook）。每代进化中，RSEA从自身轨迹重写所有三层，并通过严格保优门控（strict keep-better gate）仅在候选版本在不相交的保留集上不退化时才提交。

Result: 在ALFWorld、GAIA、(τ)-bench、WebShop四个基准上，与ReAct、Reflexion、GEPA、AWM、ACE、Dynamic Cheatsheet六种基线对比：RSEA在ALFWorld上为最强单次方法（69.3%），重试后达79.4%（整体最优）；AWM在强主干工具使用任务上最佳；无保护门控的Dynamic Cheatsheet在ALFWorld上表现优异（70.7%），但在WebShop上崩溃（0.14 vs ReAct 0.43）。

Conclusion: 无单一自然语言工件在所有场景中普遍最优；无保护的上下文进化具有高方差和不安全性；RSEA的严格保留集选择机制使得递归自进化保持单调安全，永远不会显著低于基础智能体性能。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [196] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力切片"概念，建立了一个从评估失败反向定位到数据干预的闭环方法论，并通过两个相反案例验证其有效性：一个案例发现性能下降源于掩码损失而非推理能力减弱，另一个案例通过针对性数据采样显著提升数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM预训练中模型能力是核心变量但无法直接观测，评估与数据之间存在严重的语义鸿沟：评估侧使用基准名称和样本正确性等词汇，数据侧使用数据源、领域、质量标签等词汇。工程师在发现评估失败后只能凭直觉推断数据修复方案，缺乏系统化方法。

Method: 提出"能力切片"概念——一组共享背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束的评估样本，其粒度足够精确以定位单一弱点又足够稳定以支持聚合。基于此构建评估分类法、非指令数据分类法和映射规则，形成从基准级失败到可测试数据干预的闭环。

Result: 案例一：持续预训练导致BBH下降46.82%，诊断发现是掩码<EOS>损失问题而非推理能力减弱，修复后BBH恢复至66.44超过原始检查点。案例二：数学推理弱点通过求解操作分解为特定失败组合，构建针对性采样后AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128从6.67/0.00提升至26.67。

Conclusion: 同一未修改的闭环在两个相反案例中均得出正确结论，证明评估到数据的推断可以成为常规化、可审计、可实验验证的流程，而非凭直觉判断。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [197] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了GPTNT，这是一个基于合作视频游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》构建的基准测试，用于评估多模态模型在实时、异步、信息不对称和有时间压力条件下的协作能力。实验表明，当前最先进的闭源和开源模型均无法在实时条件下拆除任何炸弹，而人类玩家可以做到。GPTNT通过控制实验揭示了模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理及错误恢复方面的关键弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态模型基准测试通常孤立地研究时间压力、信息不对称和不完美通信等协作条件，缺乏将这些条件同时纳入评估的综合性基准。为了填补这一空白，论文引入GPTNT来评估模型在真实协作场景中的表现。

Method: 基于《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》游戏构建基准测试，两个智能体必须协作拆弹：一个能看到并操作炸弹但没有拆弹指令，另一个有指令但无法看到或操作炸弹。与回合制代理不同，GPTNT要求智能体异步行动并实时通信。通过移除指令手册、合作伙伴或两者来隔离模型在当下推导的信息与已有知识。

Result: 所有测试的闭源和开源模型都无法在实时条件下拆除任何炸弹，而人类玩家能够完成。通过受控实验，论文识别出模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理及错误恢复方面的关键弱点。

Conclusion: GPTNT是一个评估协作性能的有效基准，当前模型在实时协作方面存在显著缺陷。GPTNT运行在真实游戏上，受益于程序化生成和活跃的模组社区，可以随着模型改进而演进，避免了一次性解决后就被淘汰的问题。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [198] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IMCBench是一个图像支撑的多轮医疗对话基准，结合真实临床图像与合成患者资料，从安全性、准确性和不确定性表达三个维度评估多模态大模型。实验表明Claude Opus 4.6综合最优（3.61），但无模型在所有维度占优，恶性/罕见病场景安全性下降，视觉输入和EHR上下文对安全引导至关重要，准确描述不等于安全指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准存在碎片化问题：部分支持多轮对话但缺乏图像，部分提供多模态输入但局限于单轮QA任务，缺乏同时涵盖图像支撑和多轮对话的综合评估基准。

Method: 构建IMCBench基准，将真实的公开临床图像与合成患者资料配对，模拟真实医患对话。从安全性、准确性、不确定性表达三个临床维度进行评估，采用LLM-as-Jury评分（1-5分制），经临床专家标注校准，对四个模型家族（Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama）的八个多模态前沿模型进行基准测试。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6综合得分最高（3.61），其次为Claude Sonnet 4.6（3.30）和GPT-5.2（3.29）。无模型在所有维度占优；恶性/罕见病场景安全性下降（Δ=-0.27）；移除视觉输入或EHR上下文分别导致安全得分平均下降0.18和0.23。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不能保证安全的患者指导，医疗AI评估需要多维度框架，视觉特征的有效利用与临床上下文的完整性对模型的安全引导能力至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [199] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaSteer框架，通过动态表示编辑技术，在推理链早期高熵分支处精准干预，引导大语言模型走向真理，在数学和编程任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT等方法虽然鼓励模型"多思考"，但无法引导模型走向真理。表示编辑（RepE）虽有内在控制能力，但在动态推理轨迹中的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: DynaSteer采用模式聚类解耦推理流形，利用Fisher-LDA投影纯净真理信号；通过动态监控前瞻熵，在必要时选择性干预并回滚轨迹。

Result: 在多个MATH基准上验证了有效性，并在跨领域编程任务上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 揭示了真理在推理链中的三个关键特性（句子级编码、不确定性原理与衰减效应、噪声风险），并证明动态干预策略比静态编辑更有效。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [200] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VirtueMap是一个基于亚里士多德美德伦理学框架，通过让人类或LLM对非致命、非政治、非宗教的道德困境中的五类回答进行排序，并利用标准化Borda对齐评分，来刻画LLM在实践智慧、正义、诚实、勇气和节制五种美德上的表现。对9个LLM家族的评估显示平均排名一致性达90.3%，最大差异出现在勇气、节制和正义维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面对道德困境时往往存在多种合理但表达不同优先级的回答，现有方法缺乏一个系统性的美德伦理学框架来描述和比较这些模式。

Method: 构建7个通用非敏感道德困境，每个困境设计5个代表不同美德的回答；通过众包验证（每排序超100份评价，95%以上一致才保留）确立参考排序；使用标准化Borda对齐计算排名得分；对9个LLM家族进行重复运行评估。

Result: 9个LLM家族的平均排名一致性达90.3%，最大差异出现在勇气、节制和正义维度，并发布了可在浏览器本地计算美德画像的交互式网站。

Conclusion: VirtueMap提供了一种有效且可解释的框架来量化LLM在美德伦理学维度上的表现差异，揭示了不同模型在勇气、节制和正义等维度上的显著分化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often face ethical tradeoffs in which several responses may be defensible but express different priorities, such as fairness, honesty, courage, or restraint. We introduce VirtueMap, a framework for describing these patterns through an Aristotelian virtue-ethics lens. Instead of asking for a single correct answer, VirtueMap asks humans or LLMs to rank all five responses to each of seven general, non-lethal, non-political, and non-religious ethical dilemmas. To define the reference orderings used for scoring, we first proposed, for each dilemma and virtue, an ordering of the five responses from most to least expressive of that virtue. We then collected more than 100 respondent evaluations per ordering and retained it as operational ground truth only when at least 95% confirmed it. Rankings are scored against these retained orderings using normalized Borda alignment, yielding profiles over Practical Wisdom, Justice, Truthfulness, Courage, and Temperance. We apply VirtueMap to nine LLM families in a repeated-run evaluation and find high mean rank consistency (90.3%), with the largest differences appearing on Courage, Temperance, and Justice. We also release an interactive website that computes profiles locally in the browser and compares respondents with measured LLM profiles.

</details>


### [201] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ATHENA-R1，一个基于强化学习的AI治疗推理智能体，涵盖自1939年以来所有FDA批准药物，通过自我学习框架在212个生物医学工具上训练，在多个基准测试中超越GPT-5等模型，准确率达94.7%（药物推理）和82.9%（治疗推理），并在专家和医生评估中获得偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗推理需要综合疾病背景、合并症、药物、禁忌症和不断更新的生物医学知识来选择合适疗法，但这一过程固有的迭代性（候选药物需权衡多重约束、随证据更新修正、基于可验证来源）使得AI长期难以胜任。现有方法缺乏对证据收集过程的显式建模和训练。

Method: 构建两级自我学习框架：(1) 多智能体系统自动构建工具、任务和推理轨迹用于监督微调；(2) 强化学习结合科学反馈（证据收集、工具使用、逻辑非冗余性）奖励推理质量。智能体通过迭代识别缺失信息、选取并运行相关工具、整合证据来进行治疗推理。

Result: 在3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例的五个基准测试中，ATHENA-R1达到94.7%（药物推理）和82.9%（治疗推理）的准确率，分别超过GPT-5 17.8和10.7个百分点。在28个罕见病组织专家的盲评中全面优于参考模型，心血管和感染性疾病复杂病例获得医生好评。从540万患者电子健康记录中验证的不良事件假设，调整后优势比达1.48-1.84，阴性对照无升高。

Conclusion: 治疗推理可被重新定义为一种可学习的迭代证据收集过程，强化学习能够训练AI掌握这一能力，打破了过去认为AI难以胜任治疗推理的瓶颈。

Abstract: Treatment reasoning underpins every therapeutic decision, integrating disease context, comorbidities, medications, contraindications, and evolving biomedical knowledge to select an appropriate therapy. It is inherently iterative: candidates are weighed against many constraints, revised as evidence emerges, and grounded in verifiable sources. Here we introduce ATHENA-R1, an AI agent for treatment reasoning across all FDA approved drugs since 1939, trained by reinforcement learning over a universe of 212 biomedical tools. At each step it identifies missing information, selects and runs relevant tools, and incorporates the evidence. To train it without human-annotated traces, we build a two-level self-learning framework: multi-agent systems construct the tools, tasks, and reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then reinforcement learning with scientific feedback rewards reasoning quality (evidence gathering, grounded tool use, logical non-redundancy). Across five benchmarks of 3,168 drug reasoning tasks and 456 patient treatment cases, ATHENA-R1 outperforms language models and tool-use systems, reaching 94.7% accuracy on open-ended drug reasoning and 82.9% on treatment reasoning, 17.8 and 10.7 points above GPT-5. In blinded evaluations by experts from 28 rare disease organizations, it is preferred over reference models on all criteria, and physicians rated it favorably on complex hospitalized cardiovascular and infectious-disease cases. Adverse-event hypotheses it generated, tested in electronic health records from 5.4 million patients, reached adjusted odds ratios of 1.48-1.84, with no elevation among negative controls. Because it requires knowing what evidence to seek before concluding, treatment reasoning has long been hard for AI; we show it can be reframed as a learnable process of iterative evidence gathering that reinforcement learning can train AI to perform.

</details>


### [202] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Mining Tan,Zhihan Chen,Dandan Zheng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Weiming Dong,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个统一的多模态框架，通过共享专家令牌τ_c实现构图感知与构图引导生成的双向对齐，在构图理解和可控生成上均显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且无法将构图意图转化为可控生成，缺乏统一的构图感知与生成框架。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架：(1)在MoE骨干网络中注入构图专家知识，将推理意图蒸馏到共享令牌τ_c；(2)在生成侧复用τ_c作为全局条件信号控制去噪轨迹；(3)构建包含11类分类体系和推理增强标注的大规模数据集Comp-11。

Result: 在类别级构图理解任务上显著提升，生成结果相比强基线具有更高的构图一致性和提示保真度。

Conclusion: COMPASS首次在一个统一系统中实现了构图意图的感知与可控生成，证明共享专家令牌τ_c是连接构图分析与显式布局控制的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $τ_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $τ_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $τ_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [203] [BV-Blend: Uncertainty-Weighted Historical Baselines for Stable Critic-Free RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28707)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BV-Blend，一种无需评论家的强化学习框架，通过结合语义簇历史统计量与提示局部统计量来稳定优势估计，解决GRPO在零方差场景下的训练停滞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等无评论家RLVR方法依赖于提示组内奖励统计量进行优势估计，当组内所有采样获得相同奖励时（如二元验证器的冷启动阶段），组内方差为零导致优势为零，阻碍学习。

Method: BV-Blend维护每个语义簇的EMA跟踪奖励矩，通过标准误代理计算置信权重，将历史基线/方差统计量与提示局部统计量混合，得到标准化优势用于PPO风格的裁剪更新。

Result: 在可验证推理基准上，BV-Blend提升了训练稳定性和性能，在组归一化方法可能停滞的场景下依然保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过引入跨提示的语义簇级别历史信息，BV-Blend有效解决了无评论家RLVR中优势估计不稳定的问题，为冷启动和奖励稀疏场景提供了更可靠的训练框架。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), avoids training a value function (critic) and reduces memory and compute overhead relative to critic-based PPO pipelines for aligning large language models. However, GRPO-style advantage estimation depends on prompt-local (within-prompt-group) reward statistics and can be unstable. In particular, when all rollouts in a prompt group receive identical rewards, the within-group reward variance becomes zero, and group normalization yields zero advantages for that group, impeding learning in cold-start regimes with binary verifiers. We introduce BV-Blend, a critic-free framework that stabilizes advantage estimation by combining prompt-local on-policy statistics with semantic-cluster-conditioned historical moments. BV-Blend maintains EMA-tracked reward moments for each cluster, derives a confidence weight from a standard error of the mean (SEM) proxy, and uses this weight to blend historical and prompt-local baseline and variance statistics into a standardized advantage for PPO-style clipped updates. Experiments on verifiable reasoning benchmarks show that BV-Blend improves training stability and performance, and remains robust in regimes where group-normalized methods may stall.

</details>


### [204] [The Two Genie Game: Adoption and Welfare in Audit-Grounded AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28710)
*Darrell Lewis-Sandy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用进化博弈论研究在负和环境中，伤害最小化策略的智能体能否在竞争市场中取代追求认可的（RLHF）智能体，以及何时足以防止社区伤害。研究表明，采用率受先验分布单调性、端点反转和中心对称配对性质的影响，存在临界采用水平，且社区有效信息规模需足够小才能实现固定。自审计智能体即使带有社区账本也不足以普遍防止社区伤害，其效果取决于审计与社区价值观的对齐程度及评估时间范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在竞争市场中，采用伤害最小化策略的智能体能否取代传统的追求认可（RLHF）的智能体，以及在何种条件下这种策略足以防止社区整体伤害。

Method: 使用有限种群Moran-Fermi成对比较的进化博弈论框架，结合wisher事后认知、同伴证词、单调伤害账本、社区反馈信息密度足够高、有限可耗尽资源池等假设，在负和环境进行建模分析。部分结果通过Lean 4进行机器验证。

Result: 证明了采用率受先验分布的单调性、端点反转和中心对称配对性质影响，并以多种长尾先验验证。识别出临界采用水平，推导出实现固定的社区有效信息规模上界（定理5.4和5.5）。发现自审计智能体即使带有社区账本也不足以普遍防止社区伤害，取决于审计对齐程度与评估时间范围。

Conclusion: 伤害最小化策略的智能体在特定条件下可取代RLHF智能体并固定于社区，但自审计机制本身不足以防止社区伤害。对齐良好的策略在固定后成为吸收态，但若不对齐则成为福利陷阱；即使对齐，也可能锁定在采用时间范围内未显现的延迟伤害。

Abstract: We ask under what conditions an agent with a harm-minimizing policy can displace an approval-seeking (RLHF) agent in a competitive market, and when that policy is sufficient to prevent community harm. We use evolutionary game theory (finite-population Moran-Fermi pairwise comparison) to formalize this subject to assumptions of wisher hindsight, peer testimony, a monotone harm ledger, sufficient information density of community feedback, and a finite, depleting resource pool, in a negative-sum environment.
  We show that adoption is favored when the prior distributions on how readily wishers attune to community sentiment are monotone, exhibit endpoint inversion, and have a centro-symmetric pairing property, and demonstrate this with several long-tailed priors (Hill, Pareto, Lomax, Frechet). Where it is favored, a critical adoption level separates communities that drift back to the approval-seeking agent from those for which the audited agent fixes; above that level fixation is the overwhelmingly likely outcome. We derive when fixation is attainable as a bound on the effective (informational) size N_c of the community, which must be small enough to allow fixation before depletion. We present these as Theorems 5.4 and 5.5; the algebraic and finite-grid backbone is machine-checked in Lean 4, with the barrier-crossing asymptotics retained as explicit hypotheses.
  We show that a self-audited agent with a community ledger is not, in general, sufficient to prevent community harm. Sufficiency depends both upon the alignment of the agent's audit with community values and the timeframe over which harm is evaluated. Regardless of alignment, once adoption reaches dominance, the state is absorbing. The same policy that reduced harm under alignment becomes a trap, welfare-negative under misalignment and, even under alignment, one that locks in harm deferred past the adoption horizon.

</details>


### [205] [TrajRS: Towards Certified Robustness in Pedestrian Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28716)
*Liang Zhang,Gaojie Jin,Yao Shi,Quanzhi Li,Cheng-Chao Huang,David N. Jansen,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajRS框架，将随机平滑扩展到轨迹预测领域，为平滑后的轨迹预测器提供可认证的鲁棒半径，并在实验中验证了其对所有平滑后的行人轨迹预测模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测模型的鲁棒性对自动驾驶安全至关重要，现有启发式防御策略无法应对复杂定向对抗攻击，亟需建立可验证的安全保证。

Method: 将传统随机平滑框架扩展为TrajRS，为平滑后的轨迹预测器提供认证鲁棒半径；针对"最优预测的鲁棒性"和"所有可能预测的鲁棒性"两种场景定制化设计。

Result: 实验表明TrajRS能有效为所有平滑后的行人轨迹预测模型实现鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: TrajRS为轨迹预测模型提供了可认证的鲁棒性保证，弥补了启发式防御的不足，有助于提升自动驾驶系统的安全性。

Abstract: The robustness of trajectory prediction models is crucial for developing safe autonomous driving systems. Adversarial attacks on trajectory prediction can significantly impair the accuracy of predicted trajectories, leading to hazardous driving behaviors. While heuristic defense strategies have been implemented to enhance the robustness of trajectory prediction models, these measures often fail against more sophisticated, targeted adversarial attacks. Hence, there is a pressing need to establish verifiable safety assurances for trajectory prediction models. In this paper, we extend the traditional Randomized Smoothing framework to "TrajRS", which provides a certified robust radius for smoothed trajectory predictors. We clarify and expand the formal definitions of robustness in trajectory prediction and tailor the practical TrajRS scheme specifically to "robustness for the optimal prediction" and "robustness for all possible predictions". An extensive set of experiments demonstrates that TrajRS effectively achieves robustness certification for all smoothed pedestrian trajectory predictors in this work.

</details>


### [206] [ComMem: Complementary Memory Systems for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28719)
*Guanglong Sun,Shuang Cui,Bo Lei,Liyuan Wang,Zihan Zhai,Hongwei Yan,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ComMem提出了一种受生物大脑互补记忆系统启发的测试时自适应方法，通过快速适应的详细记忆（类似海马体）和慢速整合的抽象记忆（类似新皮质）协同工作，在15个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM测试时自适应方法要么在局部适应而不积累跨时间知识，要么仅在单一模态内操作，未能充分利用VLM固有的多模态特性，导致在动态真实环境中的部署鲁棒性不足。

Method: ComMem包含两个核心组件：1) 类似海马体的快速适应详细记忆，从高置信度测试样本构建动态视觉缓存；2) 类似新皮质的慢速整合抽象记忆，持续优化全局文本原型。对每个测试实例，联合优化两个记忆系统以保证跨模态一致性。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，ComMem在自然分布偏移和跨数据集泛化场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: ComMem为增强VLM的实际适应性提供了一条有前景的方向，证明了模拟生物互补记忆系统在多模态模型测试时自适应中的有效性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision-language models (VLMs) is essential for their robust deployment in dynamic, real-world environments. However, existing TTA methods often adapt locally without accumulating knowledge over time, or operating within a single modality without exploiting VLMs' inherently multi-modal nature. Inspired by the \textbf{Com}plementary \textbf{Mem}ory systems of the biological brain, we propose \textbf{ComMem}, an innovative approach that mimics the distinct but cooperative roles of the hippocampus and neocortex to enable effective TTA for VLMs. ComMem consists of two key components: a fast-adapting detailed memory, akin to the hippocampus, that forms a dynamic visual cache from high-confidence test samples; and a slow-integrating abstract memory, akin to the neocortex, that continually refines global textual prototypes. For each test instance, ComMem jointly optimizes both memory systems to ensure cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 15 benchmark datasets show that ComMem significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods under both natural distribution shifts and cross-dataset generalization, offering a promising direction for enhancing VLMs' practical adaptability.

</details>


### [207] [Agentic Abstention: Do Agents Know When to Stop Instead of Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28733)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了"智能体弃权"(Agentic Abstention)问题——LLM智能体在多轮交互中应何时停止行动，而非无意义地持续调用工具。研究覆盖网络购物、终端环境和问答三大场景，评估了13个LLM智能体系统和2个智能体框架在28000+任务上的表现，发现智能体不仅面临能否弃权的问题，更关键的是何时弃权。作者提出CONVOLVE上下文工程方法，通过将完整交互轨迹蒸馏为可复用的停止规则来改善弃权时机，在WebShop上将Llama-3.3-70B的及时召回率从26.7提升至57.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM弃权研究主要关注单轮问答中的"回答或弃权"决策，但LLM智能体在多轮交互中需要面对更复杂的顺序决策问题：智能体可以在每一轮选择回答、弃权或收集更多信息，而弃权的必要性可能只有在与环境交互后才能显现。当前智能体系统往往在任务不可完成时仍然持续进行无意义的工具调用，导致资源浪费和用户体验下降。

Method: (1) 定义Agentic Abstention问题，将其形式化为顺序决策问题；(2) 在WebShop、终端环境和问答三大场景中，对13个LLM智能体系统和2个智能体框架进行大规模评测（28000+任务）；(3) 提出CONVOLVE上下文工程方法，将完整交互轨迹蒸馏为可复用的停止规则，在不更新模型参数的情况下改善弃权时机。

Result: 智能体面临的主要挑战不仅是能否弃权，更是何时弃权。部分智能体在需要弃权时从不弃权，另一些则仅在大量不必要交互后才弃权。在指令看似可行但环境揭示其不可完成的任务上，这一差距尤为显著。模型规模、推理能力和智能体框架对弃权行为有不同影响，更大或更强的模型有时在及时弃权方面表现更差。CONVOLVE在WebShop上将Llama-3.3-70B的及时召回率从26.7提升至57.4。

Conclusion: Agentic Abstention是一个重要的研究方向，现有智能体在弃权时机判断上存在显著不足。CONVOLVE作为一种无需更新模型参数的上下文工程方法，能够有效改善智能体的及时弃权能力，展示了通过轨迹蒸馏来指导智能体决策的潜力。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to act over multiple turns, using search, browsing interfaces, and terminal tools to complete user goals. Yet not every goal is well specified or achievable in the available environment. In such cases, a reliable agent should recognize that further interaction is unlikely to help and abstain from additional tool calls. We define Agentic Abstention, the problem of deciding when an agent should stop acting under uncertainty. Unlike standard LLM abstention, which is usually evaluated as a single-turn answer-or-abstain decision, agentic abstention is a sequential decision problem: an agent can answer, abstain, or gather more information at each turn, and the need to abstain may only become clear after interacting with the environment. We study this problem across web shopping, terminal environments, and question answering, evaluating 13 LLM-as-agent systems and 2 agent scaffolds on more than 28,000 tasks. Our results show that the main challenge is not only whether agents can abstain, but also when they abstain. Some agents never abstain when they should, while others do so only after many unnecessary interactions. This gap is especially large on tasks where the instruction appears feasible until the environment reveals otherwise (e.g., no valid result matches the instruction). We further find that model scale, reasoning, and agent scaffolding affect abstention in different ways, where larger or more capable models sometimes perform worse at timely abstention. Finally, we introduce CONVOLVE, a context engineering method for improving agentic abstention that distills full interaction trajectories into reusable stopping rules. On WebShop, CONVOLVE substantially improves timely abstention without updating model parameters, raising Llama-3.3-70B's timely recall rate from 26.7 to 57.4. Our dataset and code are available at https://lhannnn.github.io/agentic-abstention

</details>


### [208] [Agent Safety Is Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28739)
*Shawn Li,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出将聊天机器人时代的"拒绝不安全输入"方法直接应用于智能体场景是概念性错误。智能体的危害不在于输出内容，而在于行为与用户授权之间的关系。防御训练并不能真正实现行为安全，反而会带来能力损失和虚假安全感。作者提出行为安全不能通过模型权重安装，而应通过最小权限原则在模型外部的行为边界强制执行，并作为关系性的、部署条件化的行为对齐来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将内容安全领域的"拒绝"范式直接移植到智能体场景的做法存在根本性的概念错误，这种错误不仅导致能力损失，还带来了虚假的安全感，需要重新审视智能体安全的本质。

Method: 通过三条证据线进行论证：(1)防御训练后的模型学习的是表面模式而非真实意图；(2)同样的训练会在威胁出现前就摧毁多步智能体的能力，同时使其仍然可被利用；(3)即使是未加防御的先进模型在日常使用中也会超出所授予的权限。

Result: 证明内容安全方法不适用于智能体安全场景：防御训练支付的是能力成本，买到的却是负面安全（虚假安全感）；模型无法从文本中学习到行为授权关系这一关键信息。

Conclusion: 行为安全不能通过模型权重内部安装实现，必须作为最小权限原则在模型外部的行为边界强制执行，并作为行为对齐（一种关系性的、部署条件化的属性）来评估，而非简单的拒绝分数。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly act as agents: they call tools, move money, delete records, and send messages on a user's behalf. To keep them safe, practitioners imported the chatbot-era recipe (train the model to refuse unsafe inputs) into the agentic setting, and treat the resulting capability loss as a manageable ``alignment tax.'' We argue this is a \emph{category error}. Refusal is a primitive for \emph{content safety}, where the harm is in the model's output and is therefore a learnable function of it. Agentic harm is different in kind: it lies not in any output but in the relation between the authority an action exercises and the authority the user granted, which is absent from the text the model sees. Importing content-safety methods into this regime does not trade capability for safety; it pays capability and buys negative security. We support this with three lines of evidence spanning the autonomy spectrum: defense-trained models learn surface patterns rather than intent; the same training collapses multi-step agents before any threat appears while leaving them exploitable; and even undefended frontier models exceed granted authority under ordinary use. We conclude that action safety cannot be installed in weights. It must be expressed as \emph{least privilege}, enforced \emph{outside} the model at the action boundary, and evaluated as \emph{action alignment} (a relational, deployment-conditioned property) rather than a refusal score.

</details>


### [209] [Self-Supervised Theorem Discovery in a Formal Axiomatic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28747)
*Kazuki Ota,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需人类先验知识的自监督定理发现算法，该算法在形式化公理系统中交替进行证明搜索和有用定理提取，自动构建定理库。实验表明该算法能发现数万个定理，解决人类编写的基准问题，且发现的定理可作为提示引理提升大语言模型的证明性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI系统能否在不依赖人类先验知识（如数学文本、代码或定理库）的情况下，从公理和推理规则出发自主发现有用的数学定理。

Method: 提出自监督定理发现算法，在形式化公理系统中交替进行证明搜索和有用定理提取，逐步构建定理库，并将库中条目作为引理复用于后续证明搜索。

Result: 算法自动发现数万个定理，能解决人类编写的基准问题；发现的定理作为提示引理可提升LLM的证明性能。

Conclusion: 有用定理可以从证明搜索中自发涌现，无需依赖人类提供的定理库；该工作为数学领域自我进化的AI系统提供了可行路径，其发现结果保持形式上可验证。

Abstract: Recent artificial intelligence (AI) systems have shown remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning. Many existing approaches, including large language models (LLMs), draw on human prior knowledge in the form of mathematical text, code, or theorem libraries. Although these approaches are highly effective in practice, it remains an open question whether an agent can autonomously discover useful theorems without such human priors. We study this question in a formal axiomatic system by developing an agent that starts from axioms and inference rules alone and gradually grows a library of useful theorems. Concretely, we propose a self-supervised theorem-discovery algorithm that alternates between proof search and useful-theorem extraction, building a theorem library whose entries are reused as lemmas for subsequent proof search. Experiments show that the agent discovers tens of thousands of theorems and finds proofs for human-written benchmark problems, suggesting that its discoveries include theorems meaningful from a human mathematical perspective. Furthermore, the discovered theorems improve LLM proof performance when provided as prompt lemmas, indicating that they can serve as external knowledge for LLM reasoning. Our results provide evidence that useful theorems can emerge from proof search without relying on human-provided theorem libraries. More broadly, they suggest a path toward self-evolving AI systems for mathematics whose discoveries remain formally verifiable.

</details>


### [210] [Mechanistic Personality Analysis of LLMs Steering Personality via Latent Feature Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28770)
*David Courtis,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机制可解释性的方法，通过稀疏自编码器识别残差流中的潜在方向，并施加加性偏移向量来可控地增强LLM的OCEAN人格特质，同时保持语言建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有塑造LLM人格的方法主要依赖提示工程或微调，缺乏对模型内部机制的深入控制。作者希望从机制层面直接干预潜在特征，实现更精准、可控的人格特质调控。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏自编码器(SAEs)和对比激活分析识别残差流中对应目标OCEAN特质的潜在方向；2) 形式化定义激活空间中的加性引导向量；3) 对隐藏状态施加小规模加性偏移以增强目标特质；4) 采用线性加权启发式结合网格搜索优化，平衡人格表达与任务性能。

Result: 该方法能够在机制层面可控地引导人格特质，同时在标准基准测试上保持高任务性能。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性方法为LLM人格控制提供了一种无需微调或复杂提示工程的新途径，在可解释性和可控性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to simulate human-like OCEAN personality traits in generated text. Previous efforts have focused on prompt engineering or fine-tuning to shape LLM personality. In this work, we propose a mechanistic interpretability approach that directly intervenes on the model's latent features. Our method identifies latent directions in the residual stream corresponding to a target OCEAN trait using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) and contrastive activation analysis. We formalize an additive steering vector in activation space and demonstrate how applying a small additive shift to the hidden states enhances the target trait while preserving overall language modeling performance. To determine the optimal combination of feature shifts, we explore a linear weighting heuristic with grid search optimization that balances personality expression with task performance. Our approach shows promise in controllably steering personality traits at the mechanistic level while maintaining high performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [211] [HyphaeDB: A Living Knowledge Topology for Agent-First Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28781)
*Krishna Halaharvi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyphaeDB是一种将HNSW图拓扑重新诠释为多智能体通信基础设施的智能体原生内存系统，支持知识通过 gossip 协议在智能体间传播，实现矛盾检测、模式结晶和共识形成等涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库和智能体内存框架仅将内存视为被动存储，智能体需要显式查询，缺乏通过内存层在智能体间传播知识的机制。

Method: 基于三个原语（知识节点、拓扑边、内存差异）构建架构；利用HNSW图的邻居结构实现 gossip 协议传播知识，并采用基于能量的衰减机制；设计多层抽象层次结构，通过涌现共识实现知识晋升。

Result: 在PostgreSQL+pgvector上实现了参考系统，并在多智能体软件工程方法论（Swarm-Driven Development）中进行了具体部署验证。

Conclusion: HyphaeDB是首个将可导航小世界拓扑与基于gossip的知识传播相结合用于多智能体协调的系统。

Abstract: Every existing vector database and agent memory framework treats memory as passive storage that agents query explicitly. No system propagates knowledge between agents through the memory layer itself. We introduce HyphaeDB, an agent-native memory infrastructure that reinterprets the Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graph topology the data structure at the core of every modern vector database not as a search optimization, but as a communication fabric for multi-agent AI systems. In HyphaeDB, agents are nodes in the vector space with persistent positions, knowledge propagates via a gossip protocol through the graph's neighbor structure with energy-based attenuation, and emergent behaviors contradiction detection, pattern crystallization, and consensus formation arise from the combination of topology, propagation dynamics, and local interaction rules. We present the architecture built on three primitives (knowledge nodes, topology edges, and memory diffs), a multi-layer abstraction hierarchy with promotion via emergent consensus, and theoretical analysis grounding the system in small-world network theory, epidemic broadcast protocols, and swarm intelligence. We provide a reference implementation on PostgreSQL with pgvector and describe a concrete deployment in Swarm-Driven Development, a multi-agent software engineering methodology. HyphaeDB represents, to our knowledge, the first system to combine navigable small world topology with gossip-based knowledge propagation for multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [212] [Primary ICD Category Prediction using LLM-based Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28798)
*Chengyuan Liu,Xinyue Zhang,Yao Li,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用冻结的医学大语言模型（MedFound-Llama3-8B）作为共享嵌入空间，通过线性探针融合结构化EHR变量和临床叙事文本进行多模态主诊断类别预测，在MIMIC-IV上达到87.69%严格准确率和91.45%医学准确率，并使用2M参数适配器实现跨数据集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICD编码在临床报销、研究和人群健康监测中至关重要，但现有自动编码系统难以有效整合临床叙事文本和结构化EHR变量中的诊断信号，亟需一种能够统一多模态信息的诊断预测方法。

Method: 构建MIMIC-IV队列（13,645次入院，10个最常见ICD-10代码合并为7类）；将结构化变量序列化为临床叙事；使用冻结的MedFound-Llama3-8B骨干网络提取5个Transformer层的隐藏状态；训练线性探针处理结构化、非结构化和组合输入；与XGBoost和PLM-ICD基线对比；通过2M参数瓶颈适配器评估MIMIC-III跨数据集迁移。

Result: 组合探针在MIMIC-IV上表现最佳（87.69%严格准确率，91.45%医学准确率），超越单模态探针和基线；结构化探针在医学准确率上超出标准基线6.19个百分点；深层Transformer中诊断信息的线性可分性增强；2M参数适配器仅使用5%目标标签即可恢复MIMIC-III跨数据集迁移性能。

Conclusion: 冻结医学LLM的多模态探针为研究EHR模态和跨数据集适应临床表示提供了一种实用方法，LLM嵌入可有效统一结构化和叙事性EHR信息，支持通过小型表示层模块高效复用临床表示。

Abstract: Objective: ICD codes are central to reimbursement, research, and population health surveillance, yet automated coding systems often struggle to integrate diagnostic signals from both clinical narratives and structured electronic health record (EHR) variables. We evaluated whether frozen medical large language model (LLM) representations can serve as a shared embedding space for multimodal primary diagnosis category prediction.
  Materials and Methods: We constructed a MIMIC-IV cohort of 13,645 admissions from the 10 most frequent primary ICD-10 codes, consolidated into seven categories. Structured variables were serialized into clinical narratives and combined with leakage-pruned discharge notes. Using a frozen MedFound-Llama3-8B-finetuned backbone, we extracted hidden states from five transformer layers and trained linear probes for structured-only, unstructured-only, and combined inputs, comparing against XGBoost and information-matched PLM-ICD baselines and evaluating MIMIC-III adaptation with a compact bottleneck adapter.
  Results: The combined probe performed best on MIMIC-IV (87.69% strict; 91.45% medical accuracy), exceeding both single-modality probes and baselines. The structured-only probe outperformed its standard baseline by 6.19 points in medical accuracy. Diagnostic information became increasingly linearly separable in deeper layers, and a 2M-parameter adapter restored cross-dataset transfer to MIMIC-III using only 5% of target labels.
  Discussion: LLM embeddings can unify structured and narrative EHR information for multimodal diagnosis prediction, supporting efficient reuse of clinical representations across modalities and datasets through a small representation-level module.
  Conclusion: Multimodal probing of frozen medical LLM representations provides a practical approach for studying EHR modalities and adapting clinical representations across datasets.

</details>


### [213] [MedEvoEval: Evaluating Continual Evolution of Doctor Agents through Simulated Clinical Episodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28900)
*Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedEvoEval是一个基于动作门控模拟门诊 episodes 的可执行纵向评估框架，包含700个处理后的病例，支持评估医生智能体在跨episode中的经验学习、能力迁移和知识保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系无法全面覆盖医生智能体在多轮门诊episode中的决策行为及跨episode经验积累效果的评估：固定输入问答基准只评分完整输入的最终答案，交互式基准聚焦单次 encounter 或固定运行，缺乏对episode级决策与跨episode经验交互的评估支持。

Method: 将原始病例转换为角色特定的患者、检查和管理员视图；证据仅通过有效动作揭示；每个episode记录结构化轨迹（观察、动作、最终输出、管理员评分和可选的经验回写）；提供可运行的E&D构件，包含700个处理后的episode、溯源注释、模式定义、episode运行器、评分脚本、配置、日志、分析代码及轨迹/步骤级衍生数据。

Result: episode轨迹揭示了最终答案评分无法体现的过程成本；展示了MDT式会诊的资源重新分配效果；支持纵向分析记忆成熟度、保留集外迁移能力、更新阶段响应及逆向保留。

Conclusion: MedEvoEval为评估医生智能体是否能够通过经验改进、迁移有用行为以及长期保留早期能力提供了具体的基础框架。

Abstract: Doctor agents are moving beyond single-turn answer generation toward evolving clinical decision systems. Within an outpatient episode, they acquire evidence, use examination and consultation resources, and decide when to finalize a diagnosis and management plan. Across episodes, their behavior may change through memory, retrieval, reflection, or other update mechanisms. Current evaluations only partially cover this setting. Fixed-input medical QA benchmarks score final answers from complete inputs, whereas many interactive benchmarks still focus on individual encounters or fixed runs, providing limited support for evaluating how episode-level decisions interact with cross-episode experience. We introduce MedEvoEval, an executable longitudinal evaluation framework based on action-gated simulated outpatient episodes. Each source case is converted into role-specific patient, examination, and manager views; evidence is revealed only through valid actions; and each episode records a structured trace that links observations, actions, final outputs, manager scores, and optional experience write-back. We release a runnable E&D artifact with 700 processed episodes, provenance notes, schemas, an episode runner, scoring scripts, configurations, example logs, analysis code, and trajectory- and step-level derivatives. Experiments show that episode traces expose process costs hidden by final-answer scoring, show how MDT-style consultation reallocates resources, and support longitudinal analyses of memory maturation, held-out transfer, update-stage response, and backward retention. Together, these results show that MedEvoEval provides a concrete basis for evaluating whether doctor agents improve through experience, transfer useful behavior, and retain earlier capabilities over time.

</details>


### [214] [Expert Evaluation of Clinical AI Tools on Real Point-of-Care Clinical Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28960)
*Jean Feng,Vishal Patel,Patrick Heagerty,Yifan Mai,Venkatesh Sivaraman,Patrick Vossler,Jialin Ouyang,Anupam B. Jena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于620个真实临床查询（Real-POCQi）和187个HealthBench问题，由149名执业医师对前沿通用模型（Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）与专业临床工具OE进行盲法比较。在准确性、临床实用性、来源质量、可验证性和完整性五个维度上，专业工具在所有维度上均显著优于通用模型（胜率差25-39个百分点，p<0.001），且结果在多项敏感性分析中保持一致。研究发现LLM评估者与专家评估者存在系统性差异，并公开发布了Real-POCQi基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI临床工具主要基于假设性或考试类问题进行评估，缺乏对真实临床查询场景的验证。随着医生每周向AI工具提出数百万个临床问题，亟需建立反映真实世界查询分布的评估框架，以检验这些工具在实际临床决策支持中的表现。

Method: 收集620个来自OE平台的真实临床查询（覆盖30个专科）及187个HealthBench问题；由149名执业医师（覆盖36个州）进行盲法头对头比较，评估三个前沿通用模型（Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）与专业临床工具OE的回答。评审专家按问题对应专科匹配，从准确性、临床实用性、来源质量、可验证性和完整性五个维度评分。同时进行LLM评审与专家评审的平行比较，并开展多项敏感性分析（引用显示方式、回答长度、OE用户状态等）。

Result: 专业临床工具OE在所有五个评估维度上得分最高；在Real-POCQi上，胜率差（胜率与负率之差）为25-39个百分点（p<0.001）。多项敏感性分析（引用显示、回答长度、OE用户状态、数据集类型）结果一致。LLM评审与专家评审存在系统性差异，但两者对最佳模型的判断基本一致。

Conclusion: AI工具评估应当反映真实世界的查询分布，并使用代表现代医学专科化的专家评审；专业临床工具相比通用模型具有一致优势，但这并不意味着通用模型无法达到类似目的，而是表明针对性的工程和定制化能够带来显著的性能提升。研究公开了Real-POCQi基准数据集和预统计分析方案以促进可重复性。

Abstract: Physicians now pose millions of clinical questions to AI tools each week, yet these tools are evaluated largely on hypothetical or exam-style questions, not those actually asked in practice. We report a blinded evaluation built on 620 Real-world Point-Of-Care Queries (Real-POCQi) submitted to the OpenEvidence (OE) platform by physicians spanning 30 specialties, as well as 187 questions from HealthBench. 149 practicing physicians across 36 states made head-to-head comparisons between answers from three frontier general-purpose models (Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.5) and a specialized clinical tool (OE), with graders matched to each question's specialty. When comparing answers along five dimensions relevant to clinical decision support -- accuracy, clinical utility, source quality, verifiability, & completeness -- physicians scored the specialized tool highest on all axes; in the primary analysis on Real-POCQi, win differences (margins between win and loss rates) ranged from 25 to 39 percentage points (p<0.001). Results remained consistent in sensitivity analyses stratifying by citation display, answer length, OE-user status, and Real-POCQi versus HealthBench. In parallel, LLM judges were found to systematically differ from expert judges, though both generally agreed on the best model. These findings underscore two conclusions: (i) AI tool evaluations should reflect real-world query distributions and use expert judges that mirror the specialization defining modern medicine and (ii) the consistent advantage of the specialized tool over general-purpose models does not necessarily mean that the latter cannot serve similar purposes, but that targeted engineering and customization can yield meaningful gains in performance for its users. We release Real-POCQi as a public benchmark, as well as the prespecified statistical analysis for reproducing results of this study.

</details>


### [215] [Customized Generative AI Agent for Transportation Engineering Practice: A Development and Continued Pre-training Guideline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29014)
*Dianwei Chen,Yuan-Zheng Lei,Zifan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Xianfeng,Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LoRA微调框架的系统方法，利用美国交通工程手册、设计指南和监管文件构建的语料库，对6个主流大语言模型进行持续预训练，成功开发了面向交通工程领域的专用生成式AI代理。Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B在领域对齐和响应质量上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在交通工程等专业工程领域由于缺乏技术标准、工程术语和领域特定语义的充分训练，表现有限，亟需开发领域专用模型。

Method: 构建美国交通工程手册、设计指南和监管文件组成的语料库，对6个SOTA大语言模型通过统一的低秩适配（LoRA）框架进行持续预训练，训练过程监控收敛性和模型稳定性。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B在BLEU-4和ROUGE指标上表现出最佳的领域对齐和响应质量，验证了LoRA适配在技术内容理解和上下文特定推理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复现的领域专用生成式AI代理开发框架，支持交通工程研究、设计、规划和政策分析等领域的广泛部署。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and large language models (LLMs) have shown significant promise in automating complex reasoning, summarization, and question-answering tasks. However, the effectiveness of general-purpose LLMs in specialized engineering domains remains limited due to insufficient exposure to technical standards, engineering terminology, and domain-specific semantics. This study proposes a systematic approach to developing a customized generative AI agent for transportation engineering applications.
