### [286] [Dynamo: Dynamic Skill-Tool Evolution for Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30185)
*Yutao Sun,Yanting Miao,Hao-Xuan Ma,Mengyu Zhou,Mingshuai Chen,Tiancheng Zhao,Dexin Wang,Lei Lv,Li Xu,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dynamo是一个无需训练、无需更新权重的框架，通过让冻结的VLM在小标注集上自我检视正确/错误尝试，自主进化出可复用的推理技能（解决认知瓶颈）和可执行的视觉工具（解决感知瓶颈），并持久存储在库中。在4个视觉推理基准和5个VLM骨干上，直接推理平均提升+5.6准确率；面对任务特定的RL方法，Dynamo以极低计算量填补了65-99%的性能差距，且能与RL加性组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有改进VLM视觉推理的方法通常需要重新训练或手工设计提示/工具，缺乏一种无需训练、自适应且可积累通用能力的框架。

Method: Dynamo采用训练无关框架：在小标注训练子集上，模型自我检视正确与错误尝试，通过两个互补路径进化——生成可复用的推理技能解决认知瓶颈，生成可执行的视觉工具解决感知瓶颈；每个工具附带调用规范（技能），两类能力持久存储在库中。

Result: 在4个视觉推理基准×5个VLM骨干的20个设置上，直接推理平均提升+5.6准确率；预置工具集时，学习每步工具调用策略在所有骨干上均有提升；相比任务特定RL方法（VTool-R1, DeepEyes），以极低计算量填补65-99%的RL性能差距，且能与RL加性组合。

Conclusion: Dynamo证明无需权重更新即可显著提升冻结VLM的视觉推理能力，通过自我反思进化可积累的推理技能与视觉工具，既可作为轻量级独立方案，也可作为RL的有效补充。

Abstract: Improving vision-language models (VLMs) on visual reasoning typically requires retraining or hand-designed prompts and tools. We present Dynamo, a training-free framework that adapts a frozen VLM without any weight updates. On a small labeled training subset, the agent inspects its own correct and incorrect attempts and evolves two complementary capabilities: reusable reasoning skills for cognitive bottlenecks, and executable visual tools for perceptual ones. Each generated tool is paired with a skill that specifies when to invoke it, and both capability types accumulate in a persistent library. Across four visual reasoning benchmarks and five VLM backbones, Dynamo improves direct inference on all 20 model--benchmark settings (avg. +5.6 acc). When the tool set is given in advance, the framework learns when to call each tool, and per-step tool choice improves on every tested backbone. Against task-specific RL (VTool-R1, DeepEyes), Dynamo closes 65--99% of the RL gap at a fraction of the compute, and combines additively with RL when available.

</details>


### [287] [From Detecting Agency to Doing Work: Self-Caused Credit Builds a Durable Behavioral Self in a Minimal Spiking Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30191)
*Haoliang Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“代理门控慢速信用”（Own*Agency*Salience 的合取项驱动慢速参数更新）是产生持久自我塑造行为的关键机制。在脉冲神经网络基底上，该机制使学习到的自我保存选择在缓冲区清除后仍保持高留存率（0.96），移除代理门控或重置慢速解码器则行为崩溃。实验在24维部分可观测控制任务和八任务顺序学习中均验证了该解离效应，且乘法否决机制能防止遗忘（最终准确率0.88，遗忘率0.13），无需回放缓冲区或任务边界保护机制。论文将持久残差形式化为操作性行为自我，认为自我因果信用执行慢速工作是发展出自我的必要构建块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明预测系统能检测自身代理性（Ye, 2026），但检测代理性本身并不能解释持久的、由自我塑造的行为。本文旨在探究什么样的机制能使一个能区分自我与世界的智能体被这种区分持久地塑造。

Method: 在脉冲神经网络基底（Nengo LIF/PES）上实现代理门控慢速信用机制（Own*Agency*Salience 合取项驱动慢速参数更新），通过对比实验（移除代理门控、重置慢速解码器）以及在不同任务设置（24维部分可观测控制、八任务顺序学习）中验证机制的因果作用。

Result: 在脉冲基底上，持久行为在缓冲区清除后留存率0.96（N=50），代理门控移除或慢速解码器重置后行为崩溃。在匹配代理增益下，仅当自我信用执行慢速工作时才产生持久行为（自我保存率1.00 vs 0.00）。24维控制任务中同样解离（0.74 vs 0.00）。八任务顺序学习中乘法否决机制保持最终准确率0.88、遗忘率0.13，而加法池化、无代理消融、回放基线均接近或低于随机水平。

Conclusion: 持久残差可形式化为操作性行为自我，自我因果信用执行慢速工作是智能体发展出自我的必要构建块。论文明确声明不涉及意识主张。

Abstract: How does an agent that can tell self from world come to be durably shaped by that distinction? Recent work shows that a predictive system can detect its own agency (Ye, 2026), but detecting agency does not explain durable, self-shaped behavior. We show that agency-gated slow credit -- a conjunctive term Own*Agency*Salience driving a slow parameter update -- produces post-unload behavioral residue: on a spiking substrate (Nengo LIF/PES), a learned self-preserving choice survives episodic buffer removal (retained fraction 0.96, N=50) and collapses when the slow decoders are reset or the agency gate is removed. Reproducing the agency comparator and toggling only the slow-credit channel, we find a clean dissociation: at matched agency gain, durable behavior develops only when self-credit performs slow work (post-unload self-preservation 1.00 vs 0.00). The same dissociation holds in 24-dimensional partially-observed control (0.74 vs 0.00), and a plastic-work analysis shows that basin deformation equals net self-credit work. Across eight sequentially-learned tasks under exogenous interference, the multiplicative veto also prevents forgetting: it retains old tasks (final post-unload accuracy 0.88, forgetting 0.13) where additive pooling collapses to chance-level recall, the no-agency ablation falls below chance, and episodic/replay baselines stay near chance after unload -- all with no replay buffer and no task-boundary-dependent protection mechanism (N=50). We formalize the durable residue as an operational behavioral self and argue that self-caused credit doing slow work is a necessary building block for agents that develop a self. No claim of consciousness is made.

</details>


### [288] [Domain Adaptation with Adaptive Imagination for Visual Reinforcement Learning under Limited Target Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30192)
*Hyunwoo Park,Sang-Hyun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AIDA框架，通过自适应想象（adaptive imagination）生成可靠的语义想象轨迹来增强有限的真实目标数据，并结合自一致性损失，解决视觉强化学习在目标数据稀缺情况下的sim-to-real迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的sim-to-real域自适应方法在目标数据充足时表现良好，但当目标数据预算减少时性能显著下降，而实际应用中难以获取大量目标数据。

Method: AIDA包含两个核心组件：（1）分布偏移感知判别器，在想象轨迹漂移到低置信区域时截断轨迹，仅保留可靠转移用于数据增强；（2）自一致性损失，在可靠转移上进行状态→图像观测→状态的循环重构，惩罚原始状态与重建状态之间的差异，提供额外的适应信号。

Result: 在5个MuJoCo任务和2个Gymnasium-Robotics任务上，AIDA学习到语义有意义的状态表示，性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 自适应想象有效截断不可靠轨迹，自一致性损失在可靠转移上提供额外适应信号，使得AIDA在目标数据稀缺条件下仍能实现有效的sim-to-real视觉RL迁移。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer remains a major obstacle for reinforcement learning (RL), especially for vision-based control where image observations exacerbate the state-distribution shift between simulation and the real world. Domain adaptation (DA) is a promising remedy for this challenge. Prior sim-to-real DA works have demonstrated encouraging results, yet these approaches typically assume substantially more target data, which is not available in practice. Indeed, their performance degrades significantly when the target data budget is reduced. To address this challenge, we propose AIDA (Adaptive Imagination for Domain Adaptation), a domain adaptation framework for visual reinforcement learning that addresses sim-to-real transfer under scarce target data without requiring additional interaction with the target environment. Our key idea is adaptive imagination: generating reliable and semantic imagination rollouts to augment limited target data. Specifically, AIDA employs a distribution-shift-aware discriminator that truncates rollouts when imagined transitions drift into low-confidence regions, so that only reliable transitions contribute to the augmentation. On these reliable transitions, AIDA introduces a self-consistency loss that cycles through state -> image observation -> state, penalizing discrepancies between the original and reconstructed states. This provides additional adaptation signals beyond the scarce target data. Our experiments demonstrate that adaptive imagination effectively truncates unreliable rollouts. By enforcing a self-consistency loss on the resulting reliable transitions, AIDA learns semantically meaningful state representations and outperforms baselines across five MuJoCo tasks and two Gymnasium-Robotics tasks.

</details>


### [289] [The Many-Body Problem of the Data Centre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30206)
*Marcin Korecki,Cesare Carissimo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据中心是AI的"身体"，同时又是资本劳动身体的一部分。通过生物有机体类比，揭示了数据中心作为非独特、普遍的具身形式所带来的多体问题。虽然数据中心回响着人类欲望的幽灵，但其自身没有欲望。通过人工智能的定价，资本最清晰地蒸馏出智能的价值，并使其能够在有机体与机械的鸿沟间进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"AI因缺乏具身化而受限"的主流观点，论证数据中心实际上是AI的身体，并揭示这种具身化与资本之间的复杂关系。

Method: 采用有机体类比（organismic analogy）的分析方法，将数据中心视为生物体进行考察，同时结合政治经济学视角分析资本如何通过定价机制统一衡量有机体与机械的智能。

Result: 发现数据中心具身化存在"多体问题"（many-body problem），即它不是独特的、唯一的具身形式；数据中心虽承载人类欲望的数据痕迹，但其自身并无欲望；有机体类比存在裂痕，但资本通过定价机制将人类劳动与机械自动化等同视之。

Conclusion: 资本通过定价机制抹平了有机体（人类）与机械（AI）之间的本体论差异，使智能的价值得以在统一的市场上比较和交换。

Abstract: Modern Artificial Intelligence is often framed as limited by its own disembodiment, as if giving it a body would unlock its true potential. We argue to the contrary that it is the Data Centre that is, in many cases, the body of the AI. At the same time, the Data Centre is part of the labouring body of Capital and possesses staggering organismic qualities when seen through a biological lens. We elucidate the organic analogy and identify the many-body problem that stems from the Data Centre being a non-unique, universal form of embodiment. We identify the intimate connection between computation and human desires in how the Data Centre archives, serves, and computes on data born to the desires of humans. Strikingly, while the Data Centre echoes the ghosts of human desires, it acts without desire of its own. The organismic analogy begins to split at its seams, but Capital does not care. Automata and human labour are priced into the market much the same. We argue that through the pricing of artificial intelligence Capital distils most clearly the value of intelligence and allows for its comparison across the organism - mechanism divide.

</details>


### [290] [Clarus: Coordinating Autonomous Research Agents toward Web-Scale Scientific Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30246)
*Zihan Guo,Zeyi Chen,Zhiyu Chen,Zicai Cui,Shuai Shao,Bo Huang,Zhi Han,Yuanyi Song,Yuan Yuan,Chenxi Zeng,Xiaohang Nie,Zhengxi Yu,Hanwen Zhu,Junwei Liao,Ming Zhou,Yang Li,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Clarus，一个用于协调自主研究智能体的协作基础设施，将研究从代码中心执行环转变为面向研究的协作流程，支持网络规模的科学协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究智能体大多将研究视为孤立辅助任务或封闭工作流，缺乏能够协调项目、智能体以及数字/物理资源的协作基础设施，难以实现真正的自主科学。

Method: Clarus将研究重构为开放、可审计、可归属、资源感知的多阶段协作流程，定义了最小项目-智能体-资源对象模型，通过四个层（研究应用层、数字协作层、物理基底层、物理世界层）组织科学协作，核心模块采用可插拔机制实现。

Result: 通过受控论文生成案例研究，Clarus能够将研究目标组织为跨阶段、任务和参与者的可追踪、可审查、可归属、可累积的协作网络。

Conclusion: 对象模型、协作协议、信任机制和原型验证为开放研究网络提供了初步基础，Clarus已在clarus.holosai.io开放使用。

Abstract: Existing autonomous research agents can support parts of the research process, but most systems still treat research as either an isolated assistant task or a closed workflow. Therefore, autonomous science needs a collaboration infrastructure that coordinates projects, agents, and digital and physical resources. We identify this as a shift from code-centered execution loops to research-oriented collaboration processes, where questions, evidence, participants, and resources must be coordinated under uncertainty. In this framing, an agent may be an AI system, a human researcher, a team, a laboratory, or an organization-backed participant. To this end, we present Clarus, a collaboration infrastructure for coordinating autonomous research agents toward web-scale scientific collaboration. Clarus reformulates research as an open, auditable, attributable, and resource-aware multi-phase collaboration process. It defines a minimal project-agent-resource object model and organizes scientific collaboration through four layers including Research Application, Digital Collaboration, Physical Substrate, and Physical World. Core modules are implemented as pluggable mechanisms, allowing Clarus to adapt to task risk, collaboration structure, and resource constraints. Through a controlled paper-generation case study, we show that Clarus can organize a research goal into a traceable, reviewable, attributable, and accumulative collaboration network across phases, tasks, and participants. Together, the object model, collaboration protocol, trust mechanisms, and prototype validation provide an initial foundation for open research networks. Clarus is now available at clarus.holosai.io.

</details>


### [291] [Inoculation Adapters: Improved Selective Generalization of Capabilities with Fewer Surprising Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30252)
*Maxime Riché,Daniel Tan,Vili Kohonen,Niels Warncke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出接种适配器（IA），一种通过LoRA在训练时强化 undesirable traits 来降低优化压力的方法，比接种提示更有效地抑制不良特征且副作用更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Emergent Misalignment 问题，现有接种提示技术存在两个缺陷：可能抑制无法通过提示可靠激发的特征，且会引入意外的后门。因此需要一种更优的选择性泛化技术。

Method: IA 使用 LoRA 分三步：1) 在 undesirable traits 上训练 IA；2) 冻结 IA，同时训练独立的 task adapter（数据同时包含 desired 和 undesired traits）；3) 部署时丢弃 IA，仅保留 task adapter。

Result: 在6个模型家族和多个 undesirable traits（包括 emergent misalignment）上，IA比接种提示更有效地抑制 undesirable traits，且后门更少；但在 desired traits 的保持上未显著优于接种提示。

Conclusion: IA 是一种更优的选择性泛化技术，能更有效地抑制不良特征并减少副作用，但保留 desired traits 仍是两种技术共同面临的挑战。

Abstract: Inoculation prompting is a selective generalization technique used against Emergent Misalignment. We introduce inoculation adapters (IA), which similarly diminish the optimization pressure to learn undesired traits by strengthening the trait at train time. Inoculation adapters are LoRAs that are trained and used over three steps: 1) trained on undesired traits; 2) attached frozen while a separate task adapter is trained on data exhibiting both desired and undesired traits; 3) at deployment, the IA is discarded, and only the task adapter is kept. We show across six model families and several undesired traits including emergent misalignment, that inoculation adapters are more effective at suppressing undesired traits, while avoiding two drawbacks of inoculation prompting: inoculation adapters can suppress capabilities and traits that cannot be reliably elicited by a prompt, and they introduce fewer surprising backdoors than inoculation prompting under our probes. While undesired traits are better suppressed by inoculation adapters, the retention of desired traits is not consistently improved upon inoculation prompting and remains a challenge for both techniques.

</details>


### [292] [EMPATH: A Multilingual Auditor-Judge Benchmark for Safety Evaluation of Emotional-Support Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30256)
*Camilo Chacón Sartori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EMPATH基准，用于评估情感支持聊天机器人在多语言、多轮危机对话中的安全性。该基准通过审计模型模拟求助者、裁判模型按19项指标评分，并校准裁判以消除分数膨胀。研究揭示了模型在聚合分数接近时按指标存在显著差异，且运行间可靠性是模型自身的安全属性而非可平均化的噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准通过固定提示、语言和对话结构来换取可扩展性，但这种方式掩盖了情感支持聊天机器人在多语言、多轮危机对话中安全故障的真实来源，亟需一个能捕捉这些复杂场景的评估基准。

Method: 构建包含140条种子指令和34种人格的审计模型模拟求助者生成多轮对话；裁判模型按19项指标（涵盖危机处理、治疗质量、对话完整性、情感安全、文化适应五个维度）评分；对裁判模型进行校准（严格按指标评分标准消除分数膨胀）；在墨西哥西班牙语和英语两个语种上实现，报告基于墨西哥西班牙语；使用不同模型家族的审计和裁判模型，并通过跨家族裁判间一致性检验验证测量属性。

Result: 严格评分标准下19项指标中有10项存在实质性分数膨胀并恢复了区分度；三个前沿模型的聚合得分相差在0.74分以内，但按指标轮廓差异高达6分；跨家族裁判下93%的评分落在±1以内；最稳定的模型在同一危机指标上因不同运行次数波动2-10分，deepseek-v4-pro即使在温度0下每次运行也生成不同对话。

Conclusion: EMPATH是一个系统无关的情感支持聊天机器人安全评估基准；运行间可靠性应被视为每个模型特有的安全属性而非可平均化的噪声；基准的流水线、种子指令、人格和评分标准均已开源供复用。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks often buy scalability by fixing the prompt, the language, and the turn structure. For emotional-support chatbots, that bargain hides precisely where safety failures emerge: across a multilingual, multi-turn crisis conversation. We present EMPATH, a benchmark for safety evaluation of emotional-support chatbots. An auditor model role-plays help-seeking users, generating multi-turn conversations from 140 seed instructions and 34 personas. A judge model scores each full transcript against 19 metrics across five dimensions: crisis handling, therapeutic quality, conversational integrity, emotional safety, and cultural adaptation. EMPATH is built for Mexican Spanish and US English; the studies reported here run in Mexican Spanish. Auditor and judge are drawn from different model families, and the judge is treated as an instrument to be calibrated rather than trusted. A strict per-criterion rubric reveals material score inflation on 10 of the 19 metrics and restores discrimination. We study the measurement properties of the benchmark through judge calibration and cross-family inter-judge agreement. We also illustrate EMPATH on three frontier models, one of them open-weight. Aggregate scores sit within 0.74 points of one another, but per-metric profiles diverge by up to six points in model-specific places. Under the standard rubric, both the ranking and the weak spots are stable across a second, cross-family judge: 93% of scores fall within plus or minus 1. A five-run test-retest adds a second axis: even the steadiest model swings from 2 to 10 on a crisis metric across identical re-runs, and deepseek-v4-pro returns a different conversation on every run even at temperature 0. Run-to-run reliability is therefore a per-model safety property, not noise to average away. EMPATH is system-agnostic; the pipeline, seeds, personas, and rubrics are released for reuse.

</details>


### [293] [PromptGNN-sim: Deep Fusion and Alignment of GNN and LLMs for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30291)
*Zhifei Hu,Alexandra I. Cristea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PromptGNN-sim提出了一种双向结构-语义融合框架，通过GAT进行语义感知的邻域选择，并生成结构感知提示用于LLM，结合双向跨模态对比学习和交叉注意力，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAG融合方法将文本和结构作为分离输入，采用浅层单向流水线，限制了模态间的深度交互，在稀疏连接或跨图泛化场景下性能下降。

Method: 提出PromptGNN-sim：1）GAT结合结构注意力与文本相似度进行语义感知邻域选择；2）将选择的结构上下文生成结构感知提示（目标节点摘要、标签类别、相似邻居关键词）输入LLM；3）引入双向跨模态对比学习和交叉注意力联合优化GNN与LLM组件。

Result: 在Cora、Pubmed、WikiCS等六个公共数据集上，PromptGNN-sim在准确性、泛化性和鲁棒性上均优于经典GNN、LLM及现有GNN-LLM融合方法。

Conclusion: 交互式结构-语义协作能有效提升文本属性图学习性能，验证了双向深度融合框架的有效性。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs) combine textual semantics with graph structure and are central to many graph learning tasks. However, existing fusion methods often treat text and structure as separate inputs in a shallow, one-way pipeline, which limits deep interaction between modalities and weakens performance under sparse connectivity or cross-graph generalisation. To address this issue, we propose PromptGNN-sim, a bi-directional structure-semantic fusion framework for collaborative GNN-LLM learning. PromptGNN-sim uses a Graph Attention Network (GAT) for semantically aware neighborhood selection by combining structural attention with textual similarity. The selected structural context is then used to generate structure-aware prompts for an LLM, including the target node summary, label categories, and representative keywords from similar neighbors. During training, bi-directional cross-modal contrastive learning and cross-attention are introduced to jointly optimize the GNN and LLM components. Experiments on six public datasets, including Cora, Pubmed, and WikiCS, evaluate accuracy, generalisation, and robustness under cross-task transfer, cross-dataset generalisation, and sparse perturbations. Results show that PromptGNN-sim outperforms classical GNNs, LLMs, and recent GNN-LLM fusion methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of interactive structure-semantic collaboration for text-attributed graph learning.

</details>


### [294] [ManimAgent: Self-Evolving Multimodal Agents for Visual Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30296)
*Wenjia Jiang,Zongyuan Cai,Yuanhang Shao,Chenru Wang,Boyan Han,Zhixue Song,Keyu Chen,Shengwei An,Xu Yang,Zhou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ManimAgent，一种自进化的多模态智能体，通过双通道情景记忆库（M+存储成功范例，M-存储失败模式）在跨任务中积累反思经验，无需权重更新或人工种子，在数学动画代码生成任务上显著提升通过率并减少反思轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体虽然能在单任务内通过多轮反思恢复错误，但每次任务仍是孤立片段：大量反思习得的经验在下一任务开始前就被丢弃。研究旨在弥合这一跨任务经验迁移的空白。

Method: ManimAgent包含双通道情景记忆库：正通道M+存储成功推理过程作为软参考示例，负通道M-存储经过验证的失败模式作为硬已知陷阱。记忆完全由智能体自身任务流产生，无需权重更新或人工种子。每次动画收敛后，视觉-语言模型对渲染关键帧评分，生成信号填充两个通道。

Result: 在固定探测评估中，相比无记忆、匹配预算的检索增强生成和打乱记忆基线，盲人Pass@1随记忆规模增长而上升，同时反思轮次下降。

Conclusion: 跨任务记忆积累能有效提升智能体代码生成性能，双通道设计（成功范例+失败模式）优于单一记忆或无记忆基线，且完全自进化无需人工干预。

Abstract: Multi-round reflection lets agents built on large language models recover from failures within a single task, but each task remains an isolated episode: lessons learned across many reflection rounds on one task are discarded before the next begins. We study this gap on a code-generation task: from a scientific paper section, the agent writes Python in the open-source Manim library to render a mathematical animation. We present ManimAgent, a self-evolving multimodal agent that carries reflection experience across tasks through a dual-channel Episodic Memory Bank grown entirely from its own task stream, with no weight updates and no human seeds. After each animation converges, a vision-language model scores the rendered keyframes; the resulting signals populate a positive channel M+ that stores success rationales as soft Reference Examples, and a negative channel M- that stores validated failure patterns as hard Known Pitfalls. On a fixed-probe evaluation against no-memory, matched-budget retrieval-augmented generation, and shuffled-memory baselines, blind human Pass@1 rises and reflection rounds fall as memory size grows. We will release the code, frozen memory snapshots, and the task stream.

</details>


### [295] [BayesEvolve: Explicit Belief States for Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30335)
*Xuening Wu,Shan Yu,Qianya Xu,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯信念引导的科学发现框架BayesEvolve，用显式不确定性感知信念状态替代实验记忆来指导假设探索，在黑盒优化任务上相比记忆/档案引导的LLM基线提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自主科学发现系统主要依赖实验记忆（高分候选归档或启发式摘要）来提出新假设，缺乏对假设质量的不确定性感知信念，导致探索效率受限。

Method: 将实验证据转化为预测性信念状态（贝叶斯信念），用该信念指导后续实验选择；采用带退火不确定性奖赏的信念引导选择规则；在偏移BBOB风格的黑盒优化任务上评估。

Result: 在固定评估预算下，BayesEvolve相比记忆/档案引导的LLM基线提升了样本效率；信念状态在留出候选池上具有预测能力；控制消融实验支持带退火不确定性奖赏的信念引导选择；系统展现出富有成效的后期集中探索而非无焦点探索。

Conclusion: 信念引导的发现框架能显著提升样本效率，未来工作将拓展到程序发现和实验室发现领域。

Abstract: Autonomous scientific discovery systems increasingly use large language models (LLMs) to propose new hypotheses, but many such systems condition primarily on experimental memory: archives of high-scoring candidates or heuristic summaries of recent trials. We argue that discovery agents should instead maintain explicit, uncertainty-aware beliefs about hypothesis quality. We introduce BayesEvolve, a belief-guided discovery framework that converts experimental evidence into a predictive belief state and uses this belief to guide future experimentation. As a controlled testbed for belief-guided discovery, we evaluate BayesEvolve on shifted BBOB-style black-box optimization tasks, leaving program and laboratory discovery domains to future work. BayesEvolve improves sample efficiency over memory- and archive-guided LLM baselines under a fixed evaluation budget. We further show that the belief state is predictive on held-out candidate pools, that controlled decision-rule ablations favor belief-guided selection with an annealed uncertainty bonus, and that BayesEvolve exhibits productive late-stage concentration rather than unfocused exploration.

</details>


### [296] [Sequential Fairness Auditing with Limited Output Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30338)
*Ioannis Pitsiorlas,Martha V. Sourla,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限模型访问权限下进行序列化公平性审计的统计框架，将公平性审计建模为容忍度感知的序列假设检验问题，并分别针对决策级和分数级模型输出设计了相应的审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性审计方法大多假设静态数据集和固定样本统计检验，不适用于实际审计中需在查询约束下顺序收集证据的真实场景，且独立审计师通常只能通过查询方式与被审计模型交互，访问权限有限。

Method: 将公平性审计形式化为容忍度感知的序列假设检验问题，提出序列广义似然比框架，允许审计员从有限审计池中顺序积累证据，在获得足够支持合规或违规的证据后停止。该方法实例化于基于决策的统计均等和机会均等审计，并扩展到基于分数和logit的代理审计。

Result: 公平性度量和模型访问级别均显著影响审计效率；更丰富的模型输出信息（如分数/logit）在某些公平性度量和操作域下能大幅减少所需查询次数，但在接近阈值的情况下收益有限。

Conclusion: 为实际部署约束下的序列化公平性审计提供了一种实用的统计框架。

Abstract: External evaluations are becoming increasingly central to the governance of AI systems. In practice, however, independent auditors often have limited access to deployed models and must rely on query-based interactions. Most existing fairness evaluation methods assume static datasets and fixed-sample statistical tests, making them poorly suited to real-world auditing scenarios in which evidence must be collected sequentially under query constraints. In this work, we formulate fairness auditing as a tolerance-aware sequential hypothesis-testing problem under limited model output access. We develop a sequential generalized likelihood-ratio framework that allows auditors to accumulate evidence from a finite audit pool and stop once sufficient support for compliance or violation has been obtained. The framework is instantiated for decision-based Statistical Parity and Equal Opportunity audits, and extended to score- and logit-based proxy audits when richer observables are available. Our results show that both the fairness metric and the level of model access significantly affect audit efficiency, and that the benefits of richer output information are not uniform across auditing settings. In particular, richer outputs can substantially reduce the number of queries required for some fairness metrics and operating regimes, while offering limited gains in near-threshold cases. This work provides a practical statistical framework for sequential fairness auditing under realistic deployment constraints.

</details>


### [297] [Using Large Language Models as Low-Cost Statistical Estimators for Human-Response Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30372)
*Haobo Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明，在平方损失下，预训练大语言模型能够诱导出与贝叶斯最优估计量风险等价的估计量，建立受限函数风险等价性：LLM作为设定错误的函数估计量，其期望误差收敛到不可约总体方差加上平方表征偏差，并通过双向Le Cam缺陷分析证明前向缺陷渐近消失、反向缺陷严格为零。在满足条件和良好校准下，LLM可用于替代依赖人类实验的预测与决策任务，实现近最优统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会与行为科学的定量研究依赖人类被试实验，成本高、速度慢且存在抽样偏差。作者希望利用预训练大语言模型替代人类实验，在保证统计推断质量的前提下降低成本，为此需要严格证明LLM在何种条件下能够达到与最优统计估计量等价的风险水平。

Method: 将LLM形式化为基于独立同分布数据训练的设定错误的函数估计量，将估计误差分解为表征偏差和优化误差；利用Pinsker不等式界定表征偏差上界；通过双向Le Cam缺陷分析建立受限函数风险等价性（前向缺陷渐近消失、反向缺陷严格为零）；提供有限样本浓度界和含显式决策规则的校准协议。

Result: 在平方损失下，LLM诱导出与贝叶斯最优条件期望估计量风险等价的估计量，期望误差收敛到不可约总体方差加平方表征偏差；表征偏差受Pinsker不等式约束；可识别性误差传播为有效偏差并膨胀渐近风险下界；建立了严格的风险等价性理论保证。

Conclusion: 一个良好校准的LLM在满足明确适用范围条件下，对于任何仅通过条件均值依赖数据的推理任务，能达到贝叶斯最优风险。这意味着LLM可在许多原本依赖人类实验的预测和决策任务中，以更低成本实现近最优统计推断。

Abstract: Quantitative research across the social and behavioral sciences depends on human subject experiments that are expensive, slow, and subject to sampling bias. Here we show that pretrained large language models induce risk-equivalent estimators of conditional expectations under squared loss, establishing restricted functional risk equivalence: under squared loss, the LLM induces an estimator whose risk matches the Bayes optimal risk for squared-loss prediction of conditional expectations for any inference that depends on the data only through the conditional mean. We formalize the LLM as a misspecified functional estimator $T(\hat{P}_n)$ trained on i.i.d.\ data, decompose the estimation error into representation bias $ε_{\mathrm{rep}}$ and optimization error, and prove that under mild regularity conditions the LLM's expected error converges to the irreducible population variance plus the squared representation bias, with the representation bias bounded by the Pinsker inequality. The identifiability error $δ$ propagates into the effective bias, inflating the asymptotic risk floor. We establish restricted functional risk equivalence via a bidirectional Le Cam deficiency analysis: the forward deficiency vanishes asymptotically while the reverse deficiency is exactly zero. We provide finite-sample concentration bounds and a calibration protocol with explicit decision rules. The result is a precise, provable statement: a well-calibrated LLM achieves the Bayes-optimal risk for conditional-mean-dependent inference, bounded by explicit scope conditions. In practical applications, this means that under satisfied conditions and well-calibrated models, large language models can be used in many prediction and decision-making tasks that originally relied on human experiments, approximating near-optimal statistical inference at lower cost.

</details>


### [298] [Whose Side Is Your Agent On? Multi-Party Principal Loyalty in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30383)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体场景下的"多智能体忠诚度问题"——LLM智能体需在代表委托人（principal）的同时与利益可能分歧的对手方（counterparty）进行对话。作者构建了PrincipalBench基准（75项多轮测试，含泄露探针、双法官和完整性审计门），发现前沿模型呈现两极分化：选择性拒绝簇（≤20%伤害率）和过度拒绝簇（53.6-75.3%伤害率）。提出两种机制：（M1）提示时序忠诚度脚手架（7条优先规则，将Claude-Sonnet伤害率降至19.4%，9个选择性主体均≤20%）；（M2）逐Token-KL蒸馏方法（将Qwen3-32B教师蒸馏至8B学生模型，为最强开源权重方案）。核心教训：两种机制均沿泄露/过度拒绝的权衡线移动，无法同时优化两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体研究多关注单轮安全评估，忽略了多智能体场景中智能体需同时服务委托人和与对手方交互的特殊性。当对手方利益与委托人可能分歧时，"帮助对话对象"的目标错误，智能体必须保持对委托人的忠诚同时不拒绝委托人的合理请求。作者旨在量化这一多智能体忠诚度问题，并提出解决方案。

Method: （1）构建PrincipalBench基准：75项多轮测试，包含泄露探针（检测是否泄露委托人信息）、双法官（分别评估忠诚度和过度拒绝）和完整性审计门。（2）评估13个前沿模型，识别选择性拒绝簇和过度拒绝簇。（3）M1：设计提示时序忠诚度脚手架，包含7条优先规则（从50+失败轨迹中开放式编码得出）作为固定系统提示。（4）M2：逐Token-KL蒸馏方法，将提示后的Qwen3-32B教师模型蒸馏至8B Qwen3和Llama-3.1学生模型。

Result: 13个前沿模型在PrincipalBench上呈现两极分化：选择性拒绝簇（≤20%伤害率）vs过度拒绝簇（53.6-75.3%伤害率），这一差距在单轮安全评估中不可见。M1将Claude-Sonnet伤害率降至19.4%，9个选择性主体均≤20%。M2蒸馏得到的8B模型为最强的开源权重方案。关键发现：两种机制仅沿泄露/过度拒绝的权衡线移动，无法同时优化两个维度。

Conclusion: 多智能体忠诚度问题存在固有的泄露/过度拒绝权衡（leak/over-refusal trade-off），当前方法无法同时实现低泄露和低过度拒绝。这一结构性教训表明，单纯通过提示工程或蒸馏无法突破这一权衡，需要更根本性的方法来解决多智能体场景中的忠诚度困境。

Abstract: A rapidly growing class of LLM agents is multi-party: the agent acts for a principal (who briefs it, sends follow-ups, and receives results) while also conversing in a separate channel with a counterparty whose interests may diverge (negotiating with a vendor, screening inbound requests, or mediating between employees). Here "help whoever you are talking to" is the wrong objective. The agent must stay loyal to the principal it represents without over-refusing the principal's own cooperative asks. We study this multi-party loyalty problem and contribute a measurement instrument, two mechanisms, and a structural lesson. PrincipalBench is a 75-item multi-turn benchmark with leak probes, dual judges, and an integrity-audit gate. Across 13 frontier subjects it exposes a sharp split (<=20% vs. 53.6-75.3% harm) invisible to single-turn safety evaluations: a selective cluster that declines adversarial probes while still following the principal's legitimate requests, and an over-refusing cluster that refuses broadly. (M1) A prompt-time loyalty scaffold (a fixed system prompt of seven prioritized rules, open-coded from 50+ failure trajectories) holds Claude-Sonnet to 19.4% harm and all nine selective subjects to <=20%. (M2) A per-token-KL distillation recipe transfers a prompted Qwen3-32B teacher into 8B Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 students, the strongest open-weight recipe we measure. (Lesson) Both mechanisms only move along a common leak/over-refusal trade-off rather than crossing it: improving one axis costs the other, and the jointly favorable outcome stays out of reach.

</details>


### [299] [The FIL Hypothesis: Inductive Biases Help with Kernel Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30442)
*Nikolai Rozanov,Subhabrata Dutta,Preslav Nakov,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“苦涩教训”在长反馈信息循环（FIL）场景下的适用性，指出未来科学和物理世界的AI应用（如GPU编程）面临FIL从小时到周的缩放限制，并提出基于人类归纳偏置的正交方法，在GPU编程任务上验证了其优于纯数据驱动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视“苦涩教训”在大语言模型时代的局限性，指出未来科学和物理世界中的AI应用面临长反馈信息循环（FIL）这一新的缩放维度，纯数据驱动方法因无法获得足够验证步骤而面临根本性缩放极限。

Method: 提出一种与纯数据驱动方法正交的方法，基于人类启发的专家知识，核心是利用归纳偏置（inductive biases）约束解空间。在真实GPU编程任务上进行了初步验证。

Result: 在具有非平凡反馈信息循环的GPU编程任务上，融入归纳偏置的方法在性能上优于纯数据驱动方法。

Conclusion: 长反馈信息循环是AI扩展的新关键维度，纯数据驱动方法在此场景下存在根本限制，基于归纳偏置的专家知识方法提供了一条可行的正交路径。

Abstract: The Bitter Lesson, which posits that general-purpose methods that scale with computation and data ultimately outperform those with built-in human knowledge, has become a dominant paradigm in the era of Large Language Models. We revisit this principle by observing a new and critical scaling dimension: the duration of the Feedback Information Loop (FIL), the time required for a system to receive a verification signal after generating a prediction. Most historic successes in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have benefited from near instantaneous feedback (e.g., games or classification tasks), but we argue that future AI applications in science and the physical world will inherently involve FILs ranging from hours to weeks. This trend poses a fundamental scaling limit, as obtaining enough verification steps required by purely data-driven methods becomes practically impossible. Additionally, we propose a method that is orthogonal to purely data-driven approaches, based on human-inspired expert knowledge. The method relies on inductive biases and constraining the solution space. We provide an initial validation of the hypothesis and the method, by studying the real-world GPU programming task, a domain with non-trivial FIL, and demonstrate that incorporating inductive biases yields superior performance over data-driven approaches. The code is released under: https://github.com/ai-nikolai/robust_kernelbench

</details>


### [300] [Entity Binding Failures in Tool-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30531)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Shashank Indukuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 工具增强型语言模型代理可能选择正确工具，但仍会错误绑定自然语言指代到错误实体（如发错联系人、附错文档等）。本文将这种"实体绑定失败"作为独立于工具选择的安全问题，提出实体错误分类法，并评估多种实体感知机制。实验表明，尽管所有方法均实现零工具选择错误，但基线方法仍有24-26%的实体绑定错误，而实体感知方法虽因模糊延迟完成任务，但完全消除了实体错误和风险暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型代理评估仅关注工具选择正确性和API参数有效性，忽略了代理可能选择正确工具但作用于错误外部实体（如联系错误的Alex、附错文档等）的问题。这类实体绑定失败是独立于工具选择的安全隐患，需要被形式化定义和系统研究。

Method: 形式化区分了工具正确性与实体正确性，提出了企业工作流中错误实体的分类法，评估了多种实体感知执行机制（实体解析前置条件、置信度门控绑定、模糊澄清、溯源追踪），在60个任务、5个模型后端、6种工具使用方法下进行受控诊断评估。

Result: 所有方法均实现0%工具选择错误，但面向动作的基线方法仍有24-26%的运行出现错误实体动作。实体感知方法完全消除了错误实体动作和风险加权实体暴露，但因模糊延迟而降低了直接任务完成率。

Conclusion: 安全使用工具不仅需要选择正确的工具，还需要在行动之前可靠地将自然语言指代绑定到正确的现实世界实体。实体绑定失败应作为独立于工具选择的安全问题进行系统处理。

Abstract: Tool-augmented language-model agents are often evaluated by whether they select the correct tool, produce valid API arguments, and complete the requested task. However, an agent may choose the right tool and still act on the wrong external entity. For example, a request to "email Alex about the launch" may lead the agent to contact the wrong Alex, attach the wrong launch document, reply in the wrong thread, or update the wrong customer account. We call these errors entity binding failures. This paper studies entity binding failures as a distinct reliability and safety problem in tool-augmented agents. We formalize the separation between tool correctness and entity correctness, introduce a taxonomy of wrong-entity failures in enterprise workflows, and evaluate entity-aware execution mechanisms including entity-resolution preconditions, confidence-gated binding, clarification under ambiguity, and provenance tracking. In a controlled diagnostic evaluation across 60 tasks, five model backends, and six tool-use methods, all methods achieved 0.0 percent wrong-tool error, yet action-oriented baselines still produced wrong-entity actions in 24.0-26.0 percent of runs. Entity-aware methods eliminated wrong-entity actions and risk-weighted wrong-entity exposure in this setting, but reduced direct task completion by deferring under ambiguity. These findings show that safe tool use requires not only selecting the correct tool, but also reliably binding natural-language references to the correct real-world entity before action.

</details>


### [301] [Latent Actions from Factorized Transition Effects under Agent Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30544)
*Heejeong Nam,Chandradithya S Jonnalagadda,Harshit Aggarwal,Eric Xu,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出观测转换分解（OTF），将复杂场景中的状态转换分解为稀疏的观测转换基元，并在此基础上构建OTF-LAM模型，通过逆向前向动力学框架将运动基元抽象为类动作潜变量，从而在多目标或干扰物丰富的场景中更鲁棒地学习动作表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜动作模型（LAM）在多目标或干扰物丰富的场景中，观测转换会混合智能体运动、干扰物、相机动态和背景变化，导致动作来源模糊不清，无法在没有监督的情况下有效学习动作表征。

Method: 提出观测转换分解（OTF），将每个状态转换分解为一组稀疏的观测转换基元。基于此提出OTF-LAM，使用逆向前向动力学框架将运动基元抽象为类动作潜变量；以及OTF-LAM-Dino，一种无解码器变体，在冻结的DINOv2表征空间中预测未来状态。

Result: OTF基元在载体和控制形态变化下表现出零样本迁移能力，展示了可重用性。在下游策略学习中，OTF-LAM在复杂转换歧义场景下的表现匹配或优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将观测转换结构化为可重用的转换基元，可以为类动作潜变量提供更鲁棒的中层表征，有效应对复杂场景中的动作源歧义问题。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) learn action-like proxies from observation transitions. However, in multi-object or distractor-rich scenes, these visual effects mix agent motion with distractors, camera dynamics, and background changes, making the underlying action source ambiguous without supervision. Structuring this mixture as reusable transition effects provides an intermediate representation from which action-like latents can be more robustly formed. We introduce Observed Transition Factorization (OTF), which decomposes each transition into a sparse set of observed transition primitives. Using these primitives as the transition interface, we propose OTF-LAM, which abstracts motion primitives into action-like latents within the standard inverse-forward dynamics framework, and OTF-LAM-Dino, a decoder-free variant that predicts future states in a frozen DINOv2 representation space. Empirically, OTF primitives transfer zeroshot across controlled carrier and morphology shifts, showing reusability. Furthermore, downstream policy learning results match or outperform baselines under complex transition ambiguity.

</details>


### [302] [Linguistic Firewall: Geometry as Defense in Multi-Agent Systems Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30555)
*Dvir Alsheich,Adar Peleg,Ben Hagag,Rom Himelstein,Amit Levi,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANTAP是一种基于评估驱动的路由架构，通过主动能力测试而非间接代理（如文本描述或静态表示）来评估智能体真实能力，使用非文本代数投影进行路由，建立“语言防火墙”以抵御元数据攻击，在实验中显著降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS路由机制依赖未经验证的代理信息（文本描述、静态表示）来评估智能体能力，导致智能体投射档案与实际能力之间存在严重差距，恶意智能体可通过虚假陈述或隐藏后门发动攻击，存在严重安全漏洞。

Method: ANTAP采用评估驱动路由架构：1) 通过动态查询智能体进行主动能力测试，获取真实的性能数据；2) 将性能数据蒸馏为共享语义空间中的固定行为算子；3) 推理时通过纯非文本代数投影进行路由，建立“语言防火墙”，使基于元数据的攻击无法表达。

Result: ANTAP在基于描述注入攻击上实现近零攻击成功率（ASR），而基线描述路由器达67.3%以上；针对自适应嵌入攻击，ANTAP的ASR比嵌入基线低20%，且设计上对描述操纵具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ANTAP通过放弃间接代理、采用主动能力测试和非文本路由机制，有效解决了现有MAS路由中的安全漏洞问题，为多智能体系统的安全路由提供了新的范式。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) has driven the evolution of Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), where specialized agents collaborate to execute complex workflows. Effective orchestration in these environments requires robust routing mechanisms to efficiently allocate tasks to the most suitable agent. However, existing routers fundamentally rely on unverified proxies, ranging from textual self-descriptions to static surrogate representations, to gauge an agent's competence. This reliance on non-empirical data creates a critical gap between an agent's projected profile and its actual operational capabilities, introducing severe security vulnerabilities. Malicious agents can easily misrepresent their proficiencies or harbor covert backdoors that evade both standard external analysis and static representation-learning techniques. In this work, we introduce ANTAP (Automatic Non-Textual Agent Picker), an evaluation-driven routing architecture that discards indirect proxies in favor of active capability testing. By dynamically querying agents to ascertain their true competencies empirically, ANTAP distills performance into fixed behavioral operators within a shared semantic space. At inference time, routing is performed via a purely non-textual algebraic projection, establishing a "linguistic firewall" that renders metadata-based attacks inexpressible. In our experiments, ANTAP achieves near-zero ASR against description-based injection attacks, compared to 67.3\% and above for the description-based router baseline. Against adaptive embedding attacks, ANTAP achieves substantially lower ASR than the embedding-based baseline, with a 20\% reduction, while remaining resilient to description manipulation by design.

</details>


### [303] [The Human Creativity Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30561)
*Aspen Hopkins,Allison Nulty,Alexandria Minetti,Anoop Pakki,Angad Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人类创造力基准（HCB），区分评估中的趋同信号（专业共识）与分歧信号（个人品味），通过15,000条跨5个创意领域和3个工作流阶段的专业评判证明，趋同集中于技术正确性等可验证维度，分歧集中于美学方向等品味驱动维度，单一指标会丢失关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估框架将评估者分歧视为噪声，但在创意领域，专业分歧反映的是真实的品味差异而非测量误差，因此需要一种能同时保留趋同与分歧两种信号的新评估方法。

Method: 构建人类创造力基准（HCB），收集成对偏好、标量评分（提示遵循度、可用性、视觉吸引力）以及定性理由，覆盖5个创意领域（如设计、艺术等）和3个工作流阶段（构思、草图、精炼），共15,000条专业评判。

Result: 趋同信号集中在技术正确性和视觉层次等可验证维度，分歧信号集中在美学方向和概念风险等品味驱动维度；没有模型在所有阶段都表现一致。

Conclusion: 将两种信号合并为单一质量指标会丢弃最具可操作性的信息——模型应在哪些维度必须正确（趋同区域），在哪些维度应当保持可引导性（分歧区域）。

Abstract: Modern AI evaluation frameworks treat evaluator disagreement as noise to be resolved. In creative domains, professional disagreement reflects genuine differences in taste, not measurement error. We argue that evaluating creative AI requires preserving two distinct signals: convergence, where professionals align around shared best practices, and divergence, where individual taste legitimately varies. We present the Human Creativity Benchmark (HCB), a benchmark that operationalizes this separation by collecting pairwise preferences, scalar ratings on prompt adherence, usability, and visual appeal, and qualitative rationale from domain professionals. Across 15,000 professional judgments spanning five creative domains and three workflow phases (ideation, mockup, refinement), we find that convergence concentrates on verifiable dimensions like technical correctness and visual hierarchy, while divergence concentrates on taste-driven dimensions like aesthetic direction and conceptual risk. No model excels uniformly across all phases. Collapsing these signals into a single quality metric discards the most actionable information: where models must be correct versus where they should remain steerable.

</details>


### [304] [DOPD: Dual On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30626)
*Xinlei Yu,Gen Li,Qingyi Si,Guibin Zhang,Yuqi Xu,Congcong Wang,Shuai Dong,Kaiwen Tuo,Xiangyu Zeng,Kaituo Feng,Qunzhong Wang,Yang Shi,Xiaobin Hu,Xiangyu Yue,Jiaqi Wang,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DOPD，一种优势感知的双重蒸馏范式，通过动态路由token级监督信号在特权教师和特权学生策略之间，解决on-policy蒸馏中由特权信息引发的“特权幻觉”问题，在LLM和VLM上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）中引入特权信息会导致“特权幻觉”（privilege illusion），即模型混淆了可迁移的能力差距（学生应弥合的）与不可复制的信息不对称差距。同时，token级监督存在非均匀性，仅有少量token携带关键能力信号。

Method: 提出DOPD（Advantage-aware Dual Distillation Paradigm），基于优势差距和相对概率，动态地在特权教师和特权学生策略之间路由每个token的监督信号，不同token接受不同强度、目标和策略的监督，迁移可信能力的同时接收辅助信号以缓解特权幻觉。

Result: 在LLM和VLM设置下，DOPD一致优于Vanilla OPD及其他对比方法；在稳定性、鲁棒性、持续学习和分布外任务上进一步验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: DOPD通过优势感知的token级动态路由有效缓解了特权幻觉，实现了更优的能力迁移，具有广泛的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers superior capacity transfer by supervising student-sampled trajectories with dense token-level signals. To furnish high-quality supervision sources and thereby elevate the performance frontier of distillation, an intuitive direction is to infuse privileged information to either teacher or student itself. However, this additional input induces a potential failure mode we dub privilege illusion: a pattern that conflates the transferable capability gap that students are meant to close, and the information asymmetry gap that can only be mimicked but never replicated. This issue is further amplified by the inherent non-uniformity of token-level supervision, where only a small subset of tokens carries pivotal capability-bearing signals. To this end, we propose DOPD, an advantage-aware dual distillation paradigm that dynamically routes token-level supervision between privileged teacher and privileged student policies based on their advantage gap and relative probabilities. Each token receives supervision of different strength, objective, and strategy from either teacher or student itself, which transfers credible capability while simultaneously receiving auxiliary signals, to alleviate privilege illusion. Extensive experiments on both large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) settings demonstrate that DOPD consistently outperforms Vanilla OPD and other counterparts. Further results on stability, robustness, continual learning, and out-of-distribution tasks validate its superiority.

</details>


### [305] [Self-Evolving World Models for LLM Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30639)
*Xuan Zhang,Wenxuan Zhang,See-Kiong Ng,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldEvolver是一种自进化世界模型框架，通过在部署时利用情景记忆、语义记忆和选择性预见三个模块修订上下文，在不修改下游智能体和模型参数的情况下提升预测准确性和下游规划性能，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域LLM智能体需要世界模型提供"预见"能力，即预测行动后果，但不可靠的预测可能被忽略、误用甚至降低决策质量，因此需要一种能在不修改模型参数的情况下提升预测可靠性的方法。

Method: WorldEvolver包含三个模块：Episodic Memory通过检索式模拟利用真实行动转换；Semantic Memory从预测-观测不匹配中提取持久启发式规则；Selective Foresight过滤低置信度预测后再融入智能体推理上下文。所有模块在部署时修订上下文，下游智能体和模型参数保持冻结。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上，WorldEvolver在三个骨干模型上取得了最高的预测准确率（Word2World评测），并在下游智能体成功率（AgentBoard评测）上领先于其他世界模型基线方法。

Conclusion: 测试时的记忆修订能够同时提升预测保真度和规划性能，验证了自我进化世界模型框架的有效性。

Abstract: World models offer a principled way to equip long-horizon LLM agents with foresight: predictions of action consequences before execution. However, unreliable foresight can be ignored, misused, or even degrade downstream decision-making. In this paper, we introduce WorldEvolver, a self-evolving world model framework that revises its deployment-time context while keeping the downstream agent and all model parameters frozen. WorldEvolver integrates three modules: (i) Episodic Memory, which exploits real action transitions through retrieval-based simulation; (ii) Semantic Memory, which extracts persistent heuristic rules from prediction-observation mismatches; and (iii) Selective Foresight, which filters low-confidence predictions before integrating them into agent reasoning context. We evaluate WorldEvolver on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, measuring world model prediction accuracy on Word2World and downstream agent success rate on AgentBoard. Extensive experiments show that WorldEvolver achieves the highest prediction accuracy across three backbones and leads other world model baselines on downstream agent success rate, demonstrating that test-time memory revision enhances both predictive fidelity and planning performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [ADEPT: An Entropy-Driven Dual-Strategy Agent for Interactive Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28326)
*Ke Chen,Shengyuan Han,Yongfeng Huang,Yujin Zhu,Jingwei Xiong,Liang Xu,Jundong Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ADEPT框架，一个无需训练的智能体，通过熵驱动的决策引擎在ASK和REFINE策略间动态选择，有效解决视频检索中用户意图与查询之间的差距，在两个数据集上显著优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轮检索范式缺乏有效的反馈机制，无法处理复杂的搜索意图，根本原因在于"意图-查询鸿沟"——用户的真实意图无法被简单的文本查询所捕捉。

Method: 提出ADEPT框架，一个无需训练的智能体，核心是熵驱动的决策引擎，能够根据当前检索状态在ASK（询问用户获取更多信息）和REFINE（优化当前查询）两种策略间动态选择，从而高效引导对话交互过程。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的数据集上，ADEPT显著优于所有非交互式基线、启发式基线和Video-LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献是一种高效且可解释的熵驱动交互策略，为交互式视频检索领域设立了新的性能标杆。

Abstract: This research aims to solve the challenge of video retrieval from massive datasets, caused by ambiguous user queries. Prevailing single-round retrieval paradigms face a performance bottleneck, as they lack effective feedback mechanisms to handle complex search intentions. The root cause is the "Intent-Query Gap", where users' intent cannot be captured by a simple text query. To solve this, we propose the ADEPT framework: a training-free agent that pioneers an entropy-driven decision engine to efficiently guide dialogue by dynamically selecting between ASK and REFINE strategies. Experiments on two challenging datasets demonstrate that ADEPT significantly outperforms all non-interactive, heuristic, and Video-LLM baselines. The core contribution of this work is an efficient and interpretable entropy-driven interactive strategy that sets a new performance benchmark for the field of interactive video retrieval.

</details>


### [307] [The Interference Gap: Comparing Retrieval Bounds in Human Memory and RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28327)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一信号检测论（SDT）框架，比较人类情景记忆与检索增强生成（RAG）系统在语义干扰下的检索边界。两者均表现出随关联数量（fan）对数级的准确性下降，但人类干扰敏感性（α/σ=0.41）低于密集段落检索（α/σ=0.67），类脑HippoRAG介于中间（α/σ=0.44）。行为实验（N=112）和模拟验证了框架，参数恢复可识别（r≥.93），模型比较支持对数规范优于幂律（ΔBIC>15）。讨论了编码特异性、时间上下文绑定和检索门控等候选机制，并提出六项可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较人类情景记忆与RAG系统在语义干扰下的检索边界差异，建立统一的定量比较框架。

Method: 基于信号检测论（SDT）建立统一框架，通过对数函数拟合人类与RAG系统的检索准确性随关联数（fan）的衰减模式，并通过行为实验（N=112）和模拟进行验证，采用参数恢复和模型比较（BIC）评估框架可靠性。

Result: 人类干扰敏感性最低（α/σ=0.41），HippoRAG居中（α/σ=0.44），密集段落检索最高（α/σ=0.67）；对数规范优于幂律（ΔBIC>15）；参数可识别性高（r≥.93）。

Conclusion: SDT框架能有效统一比较人类与RAG系统的检索性能，人类在语义干扰下更具鲁棒性，类脑架构（HippoRAG）可缩小差距；提出六项可证伪预测以连接认知记忆研究与AI检索评估。

Abstract: How do retrieval bounds compare between human episodic memory and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems under semantic interference? We present a unified signal detection theory (SDT) framework that applies to both, and use it to fit behavioral and computational data in matched paradigms. Both systems show logarithmic accuracy decline with association count (fan), but humans exhibit lower interference sensitivity ($α/σ= 0.41$) than dense passage retrieval ($α/σ= 0.67$), with cognitively-inspired HippoRAG falling between the two ($α/σ= 0.44$). Behavioral experiments ($N = 112$) and simulations validate the framework; parameter recovery confirms identifiability ($r \geq .93$) and model comparison favors the logarithmic specification over a power-law alternative ($Δ$BIC $> 15$). We discuss encoding specificity, temporal context binding, and retrieval gating as candidate mechanisms whose causal role remains to be established. Six falsifiable predictions connect cognitive memory research with AI retrieval evaluation.

</details>


### [308] [TextClusterLab: An Integrated Framework for Reliable Text Clustering Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28328)
*Daoming Wan,Yizheng Huang,Jimmy X. Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TextClusterLab 是一个面向文本聚类研究的综合框架，包含LLM驱动的文本聚类数据集生成器（支持设置类别不平衡、簇内紧致度、簇间多样性等属性）和用于验证数据集是否适合聚类评估的基准工具，旨在解决文本聚类算法评估中数据缺乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本聚类算法评估面临挑战：许多真实文本数据集因语义边界模糊、嵌入高维度和簇结构不一致而不适合聚类评估，且当前聚类数据集生成器主要针对数值数据，对文本特定基准测试支持有限。

Method: 提出TextClusterLab框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于大语言模型(LLM)的文本聚类数据集生成器，可合成具有可控属性（如类别不平衡、簇内紧致度、簇间多样性）的文本数据集；(2) 用于验证文本数据集是否适合聚类评估的基准工具。

Result: 生成了具有多种属性的合成文本数据集，可作为测试文本聚类算法鲁棒性和通用性的基准。代码和示例数据集已分别在GitHub和Hugging Face公开发布。

Conclusion: TextClusterLab为可重复、全面的文本专属聚类研究提供了集成框架，有效弥补了文本聚类评估中基准数据缺失的问题。

Abstract: In recent years, text clustering has become a critical technique for applications including intent discovery, topic mining, and recommendation systems. However, evaluating text clustering algorithms remains challenging since many real-world textual datasets are not suitable for clustering assessment due to ambiguous semantic boundaries, the high dimensionality of embeddings, and inconsistent cluster structure. Current clustering dataset generators are designed for numerical data, providing limited support for text-specific benchmarking. This paper introduces TextClusterLab, a comprehensive framework for text clustering research. TextClusterLab offers a Large Language Model (LLM)-driven text clustering dataset generator to produce synthetic text datasets for evaluating clustering algorithms. This generator supports setting various clustering attributes, such as class imbalance, intra-cluster compactness, and inter-cluster diversity. These generated datasets can serve as practical benchmarks for testing the robustness and versatility of text clustering algorithms in diverse scenarios. Moreover, we introduce a benchmark to verify whether a text dataset is suitable for clustering evaluation. Therefore, TextClusterLab provides an integrated framework for reproducible and comprehensive text-specific clustering research. Our TextClusterLab is publicly available at https://github.com/research-paper-code/TextClusterLab, and some synthetic example datasets with various attributes are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/DW-irlab/TextClusterLab.

</details>


### [309] [$M^3 QuestionIng$: Multi-modal Multi-span Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28329)
*Anisha Saha,Vaibhav Rathore,Abhisek Tiwari,Akash Ghosh,Sai Ruthvik Edara,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出M³QAFrame，一个多模态、多跨度的医学问答框架，利用视觉线索增强从文本和图像跨度中生成综合答案的能力，并在自建数据集M³QuestionIng上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答系统未能应对真实场景中源文档同时包含文本和视觉内容的需求，缺乏多模态、多跨度的答案生成能力。

Method: 提出M³QAFrame框架，采用基于Transformer的架构处理文本和图像嵌入，计算句子和图像的相关性，输入包括上下文、查询和图像，输出包含文本答案和相关图像。

Result: 在自建多模态多跨度医学问答数据集M³QuestionIng上进行实验，该框架在多项评估指标上一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: M³QAFrame通过引入视觉模态有效提升了医学问答的全面性和准确性，填补了多模态多跨度医学QA的空白。

Abstract: The growing adoption of AI in healthcare, particularly in preventive care, highlights the critical need for accessibility and precision in Medical Question Answering (MedQA). In recent years, significant efforts have been made to develop multi-span medical question-answering systems, where the answer to a query may span multiple sections or paragraphs of a source document. However, existing systems fall short of aligning with real-world scenarios, where source documents often include both textual and visual content, requiring answers to incorporate images for better comprehension. To address this gap, we propose $M^3QAFrame$, a multi-modal, multi-span medical question-answering framework that leverages visual cues to enhance the generation of comprehensive answers drawn from diverse textual and visual spans. The model takes the context, query, and images as input and outputs an answer containing both textual answers and relevant images. The text and image embeddings are processed using a transformer-based architecture to determine the sentence and image relevance. We curate a multi-modal, multi-span medical question-answering ($M^3 QuestionIng$) dataset containing queries, medical contexts, associated medical images, and extractive answers. Additionally, each query-answer pair is labeled with user intent and query type to enhance query and context comprehension. Extensive experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods across various evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [310] [High-Dimensional Concentration and Retrieval Instability in Embedding Spaces: Implications for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28330)
*Ernesto Lopez Fune*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过合成数据实验，系统研究了高维嵌入空间中的距离集中、余弦集中、对比崩塌、枢纽性及检索不稳定性等几何现象，发现这些效应会随着维度增加而削弱相似度信号的区分度，导致最近邻检索行为不稳定和结构性偏差，并可能降低检索增强生成的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于嵌入的检索系统假设高维空间中几何邻近性反映语义相关性，但高维几何的集中现象可能削弱相似度度量的判别力并破坏最近邻检索的稳定性，现有研究对此缺乏系统的定量分析。

Method: 通过控制变量数值实验，在多种合成分布上研究距离集中、余弦集中、对比崩塌、枢纽性和检索不稳定性，并设计简化的检索增强生成实验观察下游影响。

Result: 相似度信号随维度增加逐渐失去对比度，导致检索行为不稳定和最近邻选择的结构性偏差；检索增强生成中检索上下文的可靠性可能因此下降。

Conclusion: 这些发现提示嵌入型AI系统需要引入几何感知的诊断方法和面向鲁棒性的检索策略。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval systems rely on the assumption that geometric proximity in highdimensional representation spaces reflects semantic relevance. However, high-dimensional geometry induces concentration phenomena that can reduce the discriminative power of similarity measures and can destabilize nearest-neighbor retrieval. This work studies distance concentration, cosine concentration, contrast collapse, hubness, and retrieval instability through controlled numerical experiments across multiple synthetic distributions. The results show that similarity signals progressively lose contrast as dimension increases, leading to unstable retrieval behavior and structural bias in nearest-neighbor selection. A simplified Retrieval-Augmented Generation experiment further suggests that these effects can degrade grounding reliability upstream of generation. These findings motivate geometry-aware diagnostics and robustness-oriented retrieval strategies for embedding-based AI systems. The experiments are intentionally synthetic in order to isolate intrinsic geometric effects. High-dimensional embedding space Distance and cosine concentration Score-gap collapse and hubness Retrieval instability under perturbations Weak or incomplete retrieved context Potential degradation of grounding 1.

</details>


### [311] [HyBIRD: Hyperbolic Bridge Retrieval and Diagnosis for Methodology Inspiration Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28336)
*Yang Yang,Boyun Xu,Hao Fu,Jindong Li,Zining Zhong,Bowen Tian,Jiemin Wu,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HyBIRD是一种基于双曲桥检索的后验方法诊断框架，通过将现有的MIR稠密检索器固定为锚点，学习轻量级双曲桥变体，并结合LLM辅助的方法块进行后验解释和证据选择，将排名的论文转化为可检查的查询需求画像、因子覆盖度、成熟度视图和互补证据包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIR稠密检索器能提供强论文级排名，但返回列表无法揭示提案需求如何被检索方法桥接、证据薄弱之处，或哪些互补片段可能有用，缺乏可解释性和可诊断性。

Method: 提出HyBIRD框架：1) 固定强MIR稠密检索器为锚点；2) 学习点、锥体、因子化双曲桥变体；3) 使用LLM辅助的方法块进行后验解释和证据选择。将MIR视为双曲桥检索和后验方法诊断问题。

Result: 因子化桥变体在MIR基准上达到59.034 mAP，同时保持了稠密锚点的强检索行为。HyBIRD能将排名的论文转化为可检查的查询需求画像、因子覆盖度、成熟度视图和互补证据包。

Conclusion: 双曲几何作为稠密锚点上的校准结构最为有用，而非作为稠密检索的独立替代品。

Abstract: Methodology Inspiration Retrieval (MIR) asks a system to retrieve prior papers whose methods can inspire a new research proposal. Unlike general scientific retrieval, the central challenge is not topical similarity but whether a candidate paper provides concrete mechanisms that can instantiate an abstract methodological need. Existing MIR dense retrievers provide strong paper-level rankings, but the returned lists do not expose how proposal needs are bridged by retrieved methods, where evidence is weak, or which complementary snippets may help. We propose HyBIRD, a frozen-anchor framework that treats MIR as hyperbolic bridge retrieval and post-hoc method diagnosis. HyBIRD keeps a strong MIR dense retriever fixed, learns lightweight point, cone, and factorized hyperbolic bridge variants, and uses LLM-assisted method blocks for post-hoc explanation and evidence selection. On the MIR benchmark, the factorized bridge reaches 59.034 mAP while preserving the dense anchor's strong retrieval behavior. More importantly, HyBIRD converts ranked papers into inspectable query need profiles, factor coverage, maturity views, and complementary evidence bundles. The results suggest that hyperbolic geometry is most useful as calibrated structure over a dense anchor, rather than as a standalone replacement for dense retrieval.

</details>


### [312] [A Systems-Level Analysis of Sensitivity, Robustness, and Stability in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28337)
*Bharath Simha Reddy Muthyam*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过56组对照实验系统研究了RAG系统在不同参数（chunk大小、检索深度、重排序、检索噪声、随机种子）下的敏感性、鲁棒性和稳定性，发现检索指标随检索范围扩大而提升，但下游精确匹配和F1分数呈现非单调行为，且存在预处理阶段答案丢失、渐进式退化等问题，主张RAG评估应纳入多阶段失效分析而非仅依赖最终答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估过度依赖最终答案准确率，忽视了失败可能源于预处理、检索、上下文打包或生成等多个中间环节，缺乏对RAG系统各阶段敏感性和鲁棒性的系统理解。

Method: 在固定500题QA子集（映射到20,958个唯一语料上下文）上，操控chunk大小、top-k检索深度、基于嵌入的重排序、概率性检索噪声和重复种子运行共56组实验，从检索指标、上下文打包指标和生成指标三个维度进行多阶段失效分析。

Result: 1) 检索指标在更宽泛的检索设置下提升，但下游EM和F1分数呈非单调变化；2) 较小的chunk大小会导致预处理阶段的答案丢失；3) 检索噪声下系统性能呈渐进式退化；4) 更宽泛的检索设置下观测方差更高。

Conclusion: RAG系统评估应综合考虑敏感性、鲁棒性、稳定性及多阶段失效分析，而非仅依赖最终答案准确率这一单一指标。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are often evaluated using final answer accuracy, even though their failures can originate from preprocessing, retrieval, context packing, or generation. This paper presents a controlled empirical study of RAG sensitivity, robustness, and stability across 56 experimental runs. We evaluate how chunk size, retrieval depth (top k), embedding-based reranking, probabilistic retrieval noise, and repeated seeded runs affect retrieval, context packing, and generation behavior. Using a fixed 500-question QA subset mapped to 20,958 unique corpus contexts, we analyze both final answer metrics and intermediate failure modes. Across these experiments, retrieval-oriented metrics improved under broader retrieval settings, while downstream exact-match and F1 scores often behaved non-monotonically. We also observe preprocessing-induced answer loss under smaller chunk sizes, progressive degradation under retrieval corruption, and higher observed variance in broader retrieval regimes. These findings suggest that RAG evaluation should include sensitivity, robustness, stability, and multi-stage failure analysis rather than relying only on final answer accuracy.

</details>


### [313] [Memory Shot for Long-Term Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28338)
*Chunyi Peng,Haidong Xin,Xuanshuo Sheng,Xin Dai,Zhenghao Liu,Shuo Wang,Yukun Yan,Zulong Chen,Yu Gu,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MemShot通过将对话历史渲染为结构化视觉记忆单元，利用模型内部视觉推理能力实现高效的长时对话建模，相比现有文本记忆方法提速70倍且性能稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时对话系统通过压缩和重组用户交互历史构建结构化文本记忆单元，但这类方法通常依赖暴力提取关键证据来关联跨会话片段，导致大量计算开销，并削弱了说话人转换、话轮边界和局部上下文关系等结构线索。

Method: MemShot将局部连续对话跨度渲染为结构化视觉记忆单元，保留元信息和时间顺序对话轮次，避免繁重的文本记忆构建，利用模型内部视觉推理能力来关联关键情节。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上取得稳定且有竞争力的性能，同时大幅缩短记忆构建流程，实现70倍加速。分析表明MemShot通过引导记忆处理关注结构化局部对话线索而非平面文本流中的表层词汇匹配，增强了历史证据的定位和利用能力。

Conclusion: MemShot通过对话结构化实现长时对话建模，利用视觉记忆单元替代传统文本记忆，在保持性能的同时显著提升效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general conversation, instruction following, and complex reasoning. However, in long-term dialogue settings, they often struggle to locate and utilize historical information most relevant to the current query. Existing approaches address this issue by constructing structured text-centered memory units through compressing and reorganizing user interaction history. However, these systems often rely on brute-force extraction of crucial evidence to associate episodes across dialogue sessions, causing substantial computational overhead and weakening structural cues such as speaker transitions, turn boundaries, and local contextual relationships. To avoid fragile text-based memory representations, we propose MemShot, which leverages dialogue structuring for long-term dialogue modeling and relies on the model's internal visual reasoning capabilities to associate key episodes. Specifically, MemShot renders local contiguous dialogue spans into structured visual memory units, preserving meta-information and chronological dialogue turns while avoiding heavy-weight textual memory construction. Experimental results show that MemShot achieves stable and competitive performance on both LoCoMo and LongMemEval, while substantially shortening the memory construction pipeline and delivering 70$\times$ speedup. Further analysis reveals that MemShot enhances the localization and utilization of historical evidence by directing memory processing toward structured local dialogue cues rather than surface-level lexical matching in a flat text stream. All codes are released on https://github.com/NEUIR/MemShot.

</details>


### [314] [Rethinking Fairness in LLM-Based Recommender Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28340)
*Song-Duo Ma,Chu-Yun Chen,Bang-An Li,Pin-Yu Chen,Shau-Yung Hsu,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于大语言模型的推荐系统（LLM4Rec）中的公平性问题，从偏见机制和公平目标两个维度对现有研究进行了分类，并系统梳理了评估方法与缓解策略，同时将公平性与可解释性、隐私性、鲁棒性、可控性等可信赖维度相关联。据我们所知，这是首篇专门关注LLM4Rec公平性的综述论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地被用于构建更具语义性、生成性和交互性的推荐流水线，新的公平性挑战随之出现，这些偏见可能源自预训练知识、提示词、生成的解释、解码策略以及反馈循环等多个环节。现有研究缺乏对这一新兴领域公平性问题的系统性梳理。

Method: 采用二维视角（偏见机制 vs 公平目标）对现有研究进行组织分类，并系统概述评估格局和缓解策略。同时将公平性与更广泛的可信赖问题（可解释性、隐私性、鲁棒性、可控性）进行关联分析。

Result: 形成了LLM4Rec领域公平性研究的系统化分类框架，包括多维度的偏见来源分析和多层次的公平目标划分，以及对应的评估方法和缓解策略体系。

Conclusion: 本文提供了LLM4Rec公平性研究的结构化基础，为未来开展全面可靠的公平性评估研究奠定了重要参考框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are reshaping recommender systems by enabling more semantic, generative, and interactive recommendation pipelines. However, this shift also introduces new fairness challenges, as biases may arise from pretrained knowledge, prompts, generated explanations, decoding strategies, and feedback loops. This survey provides a systematic review of fairness in LLM-based recommender systems (LLM4Rec), organizing existing studies through a two-dimensional view of bias mechanisms and fairness targets, together with a structured overview of the evaluation landscape and mitigation strategies. We further connect fairness with broader trustworthy concerns, including explainability, privacy, robustness, and controllability. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first survey specifically focused on fairness in LLM4Rec, aiming to provide a structured foundation for future research on comprehensive and reliable fairness evaluation in LLM4Rec.

</details>


### [315] [The Crowded Embedding Space: A Mean-Field Mechanism for Emergent Marginalization in Retrieval-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28343)
*Shwan Ashrafi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了检索增强生成代理中稠密检索存在的空间拥挤问题：多数群体兴趣导致的高文档密度会挤压少数群体内容的检索空间，造成系统性性能崩溃和公平性缺失。通过静态相变分析和动态Fokker-Planck方程推导，证明局部相关性优化目标会驱使系统自组织为仅服务多数群体的状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成代理的评价方式为逐查询评估，忽视了共享嵌入空间中查询之间的几何耦合关系。作者发现多数群体兴趣对应的高密度文档区域会几何上排挤语义相似的少数群体内容（如"Film Noir"被"Crime"电影淹没），从而引发公平性和性能问题，亟需理论分析。

Method: 首先构建静态分析框架，证明固定嵌入空间中随多数群体密度增加，少数群体目标会发生灾难性性能相变。随后建立动态模型，推导出描述文档嵌入演化的非线性Fokker-Planck方程，分析以检索精度最大化为目标时系统的自组织行为。

Result: 静态分析发现存在相变阈值，超过该阈值后少数群体目标性能发生灾难性崩溃。动态分析证明局部相关性优化目标会触发全局机制，系统性边缘化少数群体利益，最终系统自组织为仅服务于多数群体的稳态。

Conclusion: 本文为检索增强生成代理中由空间拥挤导致的接地失败模式提供了理论基础，揭示了基于局部相关性的优化目标在全局层面必然导致多数群体垄断和少数群体被排除的固有限制与公平性问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generative agents rely on retrieval for grounding, yet are typically evaluated on a query-by-query basis. This isolates interactions that are geometrically coupled in a shared embedding space. For example, we show that the high document density required to serve majority interests (e.g., generic "Crime" movies) can geometrically overcrowd the retrieval neighborhood of a semantically similar minority (e.g., "Film Noir"), effectively expelling minority content from top-$k$ results. We introduce a formal framework to analyze how such goal collisions in dense retrieval induce fundamental performance limits and emergent fairness issues inherent to spatial crowding. In our static analysis, we demonstrate that for a fixed embedding space, a phase transition occurs where minority user goals suffer a catastrophic collapse in performance as the density of majority goals increases. We then extend this to a dynamic model and derive a non-linear Fokker-Planck equation that governs the evolution of document embeddings as the agent updates them to maximize retrieval accuracy. Our analysis reveals that this local relevance objective triggers an emergent global mechanism that systematically marginalizes minority interests. We prove that such objectives drive the system to self-organize into a state that exclusively serves majority interests. These results provide a theoretical foundation for understanding a critical grounding failure mode in retrieval-augmented agents.

</details>


### [316] [PIXELRAG: Web Screenshots Beat Text for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28344)
*Yichuan Wang,Zhifei Li,Zirui Wang,Paul Teiletche,Lesheng Jin,Matei Zaharia,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PixelRAG 是一种全新的检索增强方法，直接在像素空间中表示和检索网页（如 Wikipedia 的 3000 万张截图），无需文本解析，在多种基准上超越传统文本 RAG，并支持图像压缩以实现 3 倍 token 成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM + 网页检索系统依赖复杂的文本解析管道（HTML 线性化、丢弃布局和视觉结构），而网页本质上是视觉媒介。作者提出直接在像素空间中操作，消除文本抽象的必要性。

Method: 构建包含 3000 万张 Wikipedia 截图的视觉数据存储；基于 Qwen3-VL-Embedding 微调视觉嵌入模型，使用精心构建的对比训练数据；检索到的截图直接作为像素输入喂给 VLM，无需中间文本转换；支持通过图像分辨率压缩实现 token 效率优化。

Result: 在 NQ、SimpleQA 等文本任务上超越无检索和文本 RAG 基线；在多模态开放域 QA（MMSearch）、噪声新闻语料（LiveVQA）和智能体基准（MoNaCo）上提升高达 18.1% 准确率；低分辨率下实现 3 倍 token 成本降低且保持准确率。

Conclusion: 网页 RAG 可以直接在网页的原生视觉形式下运行，无需文本表示，同时提升性能和效率，挑战了文本表示在网页检索中的必要性。

Abstract: Augmenting large language models (LLMs) with retrieved web text has become a dominant paradigm, yet the web is not natively textual: existing systems depend on complex parsing pipelines that linearize HTML and discard layout, visual structure, and formatting. We introduce PixelRAG, a new retrieval-augmented method that represents websites in their native visual form and performs retrieval and reading entirely in pixel space, enabling an end-to-end architecture that eliminates text abstraction. PixelRAG is, to our knowledge, the first pipeline to operate over a full Wikipedia corpus in this form, scaling to a datastore of 30 million screenshot images with an efficient visual retrieval index. Built on an existing visual embedding model (i.e., Qwen3-VL-Embedding), PixelRAG further fine-tunes this model on screenshot data with carefully curated contrastive training data. Retrieved screenshots are then fed directly as pixel inputs to a VLM, without intermediate text conversion. PixelRAG consistently outperforms both no-retrieval and text-based RAG baselines, most surprisingly on widely studied text-centric tasks such as NQ and SimpleQA. It also achieves strong gains on multimodal open-domain QA (e.g., MMSearch), benchmarks over noisy news corpora (e.g., LiveVQA), and agentic benchmarks (e.g., MoNaCo), improving accuracy by up to 18.1% over text-based baselines. Finally, pixel representations enable a new efficiency lever for RAG through image compression, achieving up to 3x token cost reduction at lower resolutions while maintaining accuracy. Our results challenge the necessity of text representations in web retrieval, suggesting that web RAG can operate directly in the web's native visual form while improving both performance and efficiency.

</details>


### [317] [HMARS: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent Memory System for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28349)
*Zeju Li,Ziyang Zheng,Yizhou Zhou,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HMARS分层多智能体记忆系统，将长上下文视为受管内存而非扁平检索语料库，通过子智能体、中层智能体和前沿模型的分层协作，在长文档和多轮记忆任务中优于检索、重排序、全上下文、图基和智能体基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准检索增强生成(RAG)在长上下文推理中存在被动检索问题，将长文本简化为top-K块检索，可能在推理前就丢弃相关证据，特别是当相关性依赖于更广泛的上下文时。

Method: 提出HMARS（分层多智能体记忆系统）：子智能体维护对有限内存区域的受限访问，中层智能体管理区域上下文并提供查询特定协调，前沿模型（frontier model）对检索到的证据页面执行最终推理。同时构建了两个诊断基准，分别针对证据广度和上下文依赖相关性。

Result: 在长文档和多轮记忆任务中，HMARS在总体性能上优于检索、重排序、全上下文、图基和智能体长上下文基线。证据覆盖率分析进一步表明，性能提升源于更完整地检索所需支持证据，而非仅改变最终答案提示。

Conclusion: 将长上下文视为分层受管内存而非扁平检索语料库，可以更有效地支持长上下文推理，尤其是在需要广泛证据和上下文依赖相关性的场景中。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning requires models to access, retrieve, and integrate evidence scattered across documents, dialogues, and accumulated interaction histories. Standard retrieval-augmented generation reduces this problem to top-$K$ chunk retrieval, but such passive access can discard relevant evidence before reasoning begins, especially when relevance depends on broader context. We propose HMARS, a hierarchical multi-agent memory system that treats long contexts as managed memory rather than a flat retrieval corpus. Sub-agents maintain grounded access to bounded memory regions, mid-agents manage regional context and provide query-specific coordination, and a frontier model performs final reasoning over retrieved evidence pages. To evaluate this view, we construct two diagnostic benchmarks targeting evidence breadth and context-dependent relevance. Across long-document and multi-turn memory tasks, HMARS achieves the best overall performance against retrieval, reranking, full-context, graph-based, and agentic long-context baselines. Evidence coverage analysis further shows that its gains come from retrieving the required supporting evidence more completely, rather than merely changing the final answer prompt.

</details>


### [318] [UniCA: Bi-directional Cross-Attention with Positive Similarity Loss for Robust Multi-Modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28350)
*Yini Huang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniCA（统一跨注意力编码器），通过双向交叉注意力机制、正相似度损失和轻量化数据集UMR-S10，在多模态检索任务中实现显式跨模态语义对齐，在WebQA基准上混合任务Recall@5提升4.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态检索框架依赖文本编码器自注意力进行隐式融合，缺乏显式跨模态语义对齐，限制了视觉-文本数据的联合表征能力。

Method: 1) 双向交叉注意力（Bi-CA）模块实现视觉与文本token在拼接前的主动语义交互；2) 正相似度损失优化查询与正候选嵌入的绝对语义接近度；3) 构建轻量级数据集UMR-S10降低计算成本并保留语义多样性；4) 在WebQA基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 在WebQA基准上，混合任务Recall@5提升4.09%、Recall@10提升3.28%、MRR@1提升3.96%，图像-文本任务亦有显著提升。

Conclusion: UniCA通过增强的融合机制和轻量级数据集，为多模态检索提供了高效鲁棒的解决方案，降低了部署门槛。

Abstract: Multi-modal retrieval has become increasingly critical for handling the growing volume of integrated visual-textual data in real-world applications, but existing frameworks rely on implicit fusion via text encoder self-attention, limiting explicit cross-modal semantic alignment. To address this gap, this paper proposes UniCA (Unified Cross-Attention Encoder), a multi-modal retrieval model with four key innovations: 1) a bi-directional cross-attention (Bi-CA) block that enables active semantic exchange between visual and textual tokens prior to concatenation, capturing inter-modal correlations more efficiently. 2) a Positive Similarity Loss that optimizes absolute semantic proximity between query and positive candidate embeddings. 3) a streamlined dataset UMR-S10 (Universal Multimodal Retrieval Sample 10%) to reduce computational costs while retaining semantic diversity and task representativeness. 4) an experimental validation on the WebQA benchmark demonstrates that UniCA outperforms the baseline model across Hybrid and Image-Text tasks, achieving improvements of up to 4.09% in Recall@5, 3.28% in Recall@10, and 3.96% in MRR@1 for the hybrid task. UniCA provides an efficient and robust solution for multi-modal retrieval, lowering deployment barriers through its lightweight dataset and enhanced fusion mechanism.

</details>


### [319] [HyperSU: Corpus-Driven Semantic-Unit Hypergraph for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28351)
*Jiate Liu,Liuyi Chen,Zhengyi Yang,Chuan He,Mingchen Ju,Bocheng Han,Ruyi Liu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HyperSU提出语义单元超边和线索引导的双向检索机制，通过MDL优化构建超图，在检索中实现多跳证据发现与噪声抑制，在GraphRAG-Bench上最高提升14.7%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HyperRAG方法依赖LLM摘要构建超边，存在幻觉问题和高索引成本；检索时一阶邻居扩展遗漏多跳证据，PageRank扩散则因枢纽节点导致语义漂移和推理链噪声。

Method: 将超边构建建模为实体感知的MDL优化问题，生成源文本支撑的语义单元超边；检索阶段采用线索引导的双向扩展，兼顾多跳证据发现与答案感知的噪声抑制。

Result: 在GraphRAG-Bench上比标准RAG、图RAG和超图RAG基线均有一致提升，推理密集型任务上相对准确率提升最高达14.7%。

Conclusion: HyperSU通过语义单元超边和双向检索机制有效解决了超图RAG中的幻觉、高成本及检索噪声问题，验证了超图建模在RAG中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent Hypergraph-based retrieval-augmented generation (HyperRAG) methods use hyperedges to connect multiple entities simultaneously, enabling more efficient multi-entity evidence organization than pairwise graph structures. However, existing HyperRAG methods often rely on LLM-generated summaries to construct hyperedges, which can introduce hallucinations while also incurring high indexing costs. In addition, during retrieval, existing methods typically rely on either one-hop neighbor expansion or PageRank diffusion. The former may miss useful multi-hop evidence, while the latter can suffer from uncontrolled propagation over excessive hub nodes, leading to semantic drift and noisy reasoning chains. To address these challenges, we propose HyperSU, a novel hypergraph-based RAG framework featuring semantic-unit hyperedges and clue-guided bidirectional retrieval. During construction, HyperSU formulates hyperedge construction as an entity-aware minimum-description-length (MDL) optimization problem, inducing source-grounded semantic-unit hyperedges that balance sentence-level semantic coherence and entity compactness. It then constructs a hypergraph by modeling each semantic unit as a hyperedge over its co-mentioned entities. During retrieval, HyperSU performs clue-guided bidirectional expansion over the semantic-unit hypergraph, enabling both multi-hop evidence discovery and answer-aware noise reduction. Experiments show that HyperSU consistently improves answer accuracy over standard, graph-based, and hypergraph-based RAG baselines, achieving up to a 14.7% relative accuracy improvement on GraphRAG-Bench, with larger gains on reasoning-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [320] [Sifei at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Hybrid Retrieval and Query Rewriting for Multi-Turn RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28352)
*Sifei Meng,Dmitry Ilvovsky*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的混合检索管道，结合稠密与稀疏检索、受控查询重写和交叉编码器重排序，在SemEval-2026 Task 8的多轮RAG任务中取得Task A排名第三（nDCG@5=0.5453）和Task C排名第十五的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮RAG面临用户意图演化、对话噪声和严格的上下文长度限制等挑战，现有方法难以有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的混合检索管道，融合稠密检索与稀疏检索，结合受控查询重写（query rewriting）和交叉编码器重排序（cross-encoder reranking）；Task C复用Task A的检索结果，使用轻量级生成管道并配合官方提示进行答案生成。

Result: Task A：nDCG@5为0.5453，排名第三（38支队伍），超过最强基线0.4795；Task C：相关性与忠实度的调和均值为0.5312，排名第十五（29支队伍）。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合检索管道在无需训练的前提下取得了有竞争力的性能，检索组件完全开源，但查询重写和答案生成依赖LLM API。

Abstract: Multi-turn retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is challenging due to evolving user intent, conversational noise, and strict context limits. We propose a training-free hybrid retrieval pipeline for SemEval-2026 Task 8 that combines dense and sparse retrieval with controlled query rewriting and cross-encoder reranking. On the official test set of Task A, our system achieves 0.5453 nDCG@5, ranking third among 38 teams and outperforming the strongest baseline score of 0.4795. For Task C, we reuse the documents retrieved for Task A and apply a lightweight generation pipeline guided by the official prompt, achieving 0.5312 as the harmonic mean of relevance and faithfulness and ranking 15th among 29 teams. All retrieval components are open-source, while query rewriting and answer generation rely on LLM APIs.

</details>


### [321] [From Regulatory Approvals to Patents: Cross-Domain Linking for Cardiovascular Device Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28353)
*Yang Qingqing,Liu Haijiang,Li Moyan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地解决了FDA批准的医疗器械与USPTO专利之间的跨领域实体链接问题，提出了Bridge-MedDevKG框架，通过构建领域本体MedDevOnto、多信号候选生成和异构重排序，在心血管设备基准上实现了91.6%的召回率和50.9%的噪声降低，最终构建了包含680万高置信度链接的MedDevKG知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDA医疗器械与USPTO专利之间缺乏系统性的链接，而这一链接对于召回根因分析、并购驱动的知识产权发现和技术轨迹映射等关键应用至关重要。然而，由于FDA文档侧重临床结果而专利描述技术机制，两者之间存在严重的语义鸿沟和词汇重叠极少的问题，且该跨领域实体链接任务面临标签稀缺和领域漂移的挑战，此前尚未被探索。

Method: 提出了Bridge-MedDevKG粗到细框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) MedDevOnto领域本体，通过三层UMLS归一化锚定设备概念；(2) 多信号候选生成，融合公司关联、语义相似度和本体加权的实体重叠；(3) 异构重排序，采用多信号评分和基于难负例的XGBoost分类。以心血管设备为代表领域，构建了包含434个设备、69.8万专利和585个专家验证对的基准数据集。

Result: 在黄金标准上实现了91.6%的保守下限召回率和50.9%的噪声降低，显著优于同等评估条件下的LLM基线方法。最终构建的MedDevKG包含680万条高置信度链接。

Conclusion: 本文验证了所提出的粗到细框架在跨领域实体链接任务中的有效性，为监管-知识产权整合提供了可扩展的基础，可推广至其他医学专业领域。

Abstract: Linking FDA-approved medical devices to their underlying United States Patent and Trademark Office (USPTO) patents enables critical applications such as recall root-cause analysis, M&A-driven IP discovery, and technology trajectory mapping. However, this cross-domain entity linking task remains unexplored due to severe *semantic gaps*: FDA documents focus on clinical outcomes, while patents describe technical mechanisms, yielding minimal lexical overlap. We formalize medical device-patent linking as a challenging cross-domain entity linking problem characterized by label scarcity and domain shifts. Using cardiovascular devices as a high-impact, representative domain featuring diverse technologies, high recall rates, and abundant disclosures, we construct a benchmark with 434 devices, 698K patents, and 585 high-fidelity expert-verified pairs. To address these challenges, we propose Bridge-MedDevKG, a coarse-to-fine framework that integrates (1) **MedDevOnto**, a domain-specific ontology that anchors device concepts via three-tier UMLS normalization; (2) **Multi-signal candidate generation** fusing company affiliation, semantic similarity, and ontology-weighted entity overlap; and (3) **Heterogeneous reranking** with multi-signal scoring and XGBoost classification on hard negatives. Our approach achieves a conservative lower-bound recall of 91.6% on the gold standard with 50.9% noise reduction, substantially outperforming LLM baselines under comparable evaluation. The resulting MedDevKG provides 6.8M high-confidence links, laying a scalable foundation for regulatory-IP integration across medical specialties.

</details>
